Nashr user journey: every step

303 customer steps plus 32 founder-console and repeated steps, traced in the code at commit f0e7fde, from the first visit to daily use. Each row: what the person does, what they see, what the system does, and the exact lines of code. Model calls are paused in this build, so rows say what the person sees then.

← journey index

  1. 01 Discover: land and browse the website 20
  2. 02 Discover: try tools, send forms, start 18
  3. 03 Sign up, sign in and join a team 22
  4. 04 First run: confirm the site and build the workspace 18
  5. 05 First run: starter plan, launch path and files 16
  6. 06 Create: chat with the agents 29
  7. 07 Create: brand facts, threads, Generate, inspiration, calendar 14
  8. 08 Review: the approval inbox 23
  9. 09 Review: images, video and slides 10
  10. 10 Publish: connect platforms and post 13

01 Discover: land and browse the websiteopen diagram →

#StepThe person doesThey seeThe system doesCode
1
Open the site
visitor
/ (src/proxy.ts)
Types the bare domain or opens a link to "/".The browser lands on /ar. It lands on /en instead when the browser's first language is English and the list does not start with Arabic, or on the language the person picked before. Any query string is kept.The proxy reads the nashr_lang cookie, then Accept-Language, defaults to Arabic, and answers 307 to /{lang}. Nothing is stored. The proxy matcher covers only "/" and /:lang/app/*, so marketing pages are served without it.
  • cookie nashr_lang=ar|en wins
  • Accept-Language starts with en and not ar → /en
  • otherwise → /ar
src/proxy.ts:5–13
src/proxy.ts:18–24
src/proxy.ts:32
2
See the home page
visitor
/{lang}
Reads the home page and scrolls through it, or follows an anchor link in the nav.The page renders right to left in Arabic and left to right in English, under the public name Thakeen (ذكين). In order: the nav (the wordmark, six anchor links: Team, Rules, Your day, How Thakeen works, Plans and Questions, then the language pill, a Log in link and a ghost 'Start free' button); the cover, with an 'Issue No. 1' stamp, the wordmark with 'Smart. Steadfast.' boxed beside it, the offer 'Eight agents who know your brand.' and 'They do the work, you approve.', a hint to hover over or tap a figure, and the lineup of the eight agents and the owner; 'Team rules', four short chats between agents, each pinned with the rule it shows; 'Your day', four panels from 7:58 a.m. to the owner's one word, 'Approved.'; 'What happens after you paste the link?', seven steps (Your brand, Your words, Plan, Draft, Your approval, Analysis, Measurement), each with the line its agent says; 'Plans.' with the Starter, Pro, Business and Agency cards; 'Questions before you start' with three questions; and the footer. While the model is paused, a 'Today:' note sits under steps 1, 3, 4, 5 and 6. There is no URL box on the page: the ways to start are the nav button and the plan buttons.A static server render. src/app/[lang]/page.tsx composes Cover, Rules, Day, Steps, Plans and HomeFaq from the copy in src/lib/home.ts and the dictionary. Steps reads CLAUDE_PAUSED (src/lib/model.ts, currently true, so the notes show) and shows each paused note only while it is true. Plans builds its cards from PUBLIC_TIERS and the dictionary's pricing block, which reads src/lib/pricing.ts, so no price is typed in the page. The title and description come from SITE_DEFAULT (the slogan) in src/lib/page-meta.ts; the language layout adds the base URL, hreflang and share-card fields, and calls notFound() for any prefix other than ar or en. Nothing is read from Redis and no model is called.
  • unknown language prefix → notFound (404)
  • model paused (CLAUDE_PAUSED true) → 'Today:' notes under steps 1, 3, 4, 5, 6
  • model turned back on → no notes

3
Meet the team in the lineup
visitor
/{lang} cover (Lineup)
Hovers over, tabs to or taps one of the nine figures (the eight agents and the owner). May press 'Pause motion', then Escape or a tap elsewhere.A thought cloud opens above the figure's head with one of its questions, for example Noor: 'What do I post today?'. Each new hover, focus or tap shows that agent's next question, and the owner's cloud says 'Approved.'. The chosen figure grows from its feet, nothing around it moves, and the name tag stays under it. Under the owner a caption reads '…and this is you. You have one word to say.' A permanent 'AI agents' label discloses what the figures are. A hover lets go 450 ms after the pointer leaves. A tap keeps the figure chosen until it is tapped again, another is chosen, Escape is pressed or the page is tapped elsewhere. Keyboard focus and taps are also read out through a polite live region; a passing mouse is silent. The 'Pause motion' button turns into 'Play motion' and stops the page's drifting drawings.Client only (Lineup.tsx). The questions come from AGENT_CAST in src/lib/home.ts: each maps to a task type its agent really performs, or, for Reem, to her to-do list. They rotate in order, per figure. The motion switch is one in-memory flag in motion.ts, written by the single button in the lineup's head; it sets data-motion=off on the page for this visit and stores nothing. Without JavaScript the button is hidden. No request is sent and no model is called.
  • pointer or keyboard focus → cloud
  • tap → figure stays chosen
  • Escape or tap outside → cloud closes
  • Pause motion → page motion stops
src/components/home/Lineup.tsx:31–69
src/components/home/Lineup.tsx:120–190
src/components/home/MotionToggle.tsx:12–20
src/components/home/motion.ts:18–25
src/lib/home.ts:70–178
4
Switch language
visitor
LangToggle in the nav and footer, every public page
Clicks عربي or EN in the language pill.The same page opens in the other language and keeps the query and the hash. Clicking the language already shown does nothing.Client only. It sets the cookie nashr_lang={lang} for one year (path=/, samesite=lax) and swaps the /ar or /en prefix with router.push. After that, the "/" redirect uses the cookie. The pill is a plain link to the swapped path, so a click that lands before the page hydrates follows that link: no cookie is set and the query is dropped.
  • same language → no-op
  • clicked before hydration → plain link, no cookie
src/components/LangToggle.tsx:7–9
src/components/LangToggle.tsx:12–16
src/components/LangToggle.tsx:22–27
src/components/LangToggle.tsx:33–45
src/components/Nav.tsx:73
src/components/Footer.tsx:39
5
Browse menus and footer
visitor
Nav and Footer on every public page
Clicks the wordmark, follows an anchor link on the home page, hovers over or focuses the Agents, Industries and Resources dropdowns on another page, clicks Customers, Agencies or Pricing, opens the mobile menu, or clicks a footer link.The wordmark goes to /{lang}. On the home page the desktop nav shows six in-page anchor links in place of the menus: Team, Rules, Your day, How Thakeen works, Plans and Questions, each with a hand-drawn underline on hover or focus. On every other public page it shows: the Agents dropdown (its label links to /{lang}/agents/noor; the menu lists the 8 agents plus 'The whole team', /agents); the Industries dropdown (its label links to /{lang}/customers; the menu lists the 7 industry pages); Customers; Agencies ('الوكالات' in Arabic, opening /{lang}/enterprise); the Resources dropdown (its label goes to /{lang}/resources; the menu lists Blog and guides, Free tools, Help center, 'What is an AI marketing agent?', Affiliate program, and Updates & wishlist); and Pricing. The dropdowns open on hover or keyboard focus without JavaScript. On the right: the language pill, a Log in link (hidden on the narrowest screens) and a ghost 'Start free' button to signup. Below the lg breakpoint a hamburger (a <details> menu) opens a list: on the home page the six anchors first, then the Agents, Industries and Resources groups, then Customers, Agencies, Pricing and Log in. The footer lists every agent (name · role), every industry and every resource, then Customers, Agencies, Pricing, Comparisons (/resources#compare), Privacy and Terms, with the wordmark, a tagline and a language pill.Plain links and in-page anchors. No server call. The anchors come from HOME_NAV (src/lib/home.ts) and are passed to Nav only by the home page. The labels come from SITE[lang].menu.
  • home page → anchor links replace the desktop menus
  • other pages → desktop dropdowns (lg and up)
  • mobile hamburger (below lg)

6
Read the FAQ
visitor
/{lang}#faq (HomeFaq) and /{lang}/pricing (Faq); FAQ blocks on the industry, comparison and AI-agent pages
Clicks a question.The answer expands under the question. On the home page there are three questions: 'Does anything get published without my approval?', 'I approved… does it go out right away?' and 'What do you do with my data?'. Each has a hand-drawn plus that turns into a minus when open and a bracket beside the answer. On the pricing, industry, comparison and definition pages the + turns 45 degrees and reads as an ×. On the pricing FAQ, 'How much does it cost?' answers: Starter 299, Pro 499, Business 999 SAR, and Agency by contract; a post is 1 credit, an article 2, a board deck 5; chat with the team is included up to the plan's monthly messages (Starter 250, Pro 650, Business 1,300, 30 for the whole trial); when they run out, chat pauses until the 1st (UTC), or on the trial until you pick a plan, unless the owner of a paid plan turns on continuing on credits, where each extra message is charged its real cost, at most 5 credits, and needs at least 5 in the balance; setting up the first brand is free and each added brand from its URL is 9 credits.Native <details> elements. No script and no server call. The home answers are static copy (HOME_FAQ in src/lib/home.ts). The price answer on the pricing FAQ is built from PLAN_PRICE_SAR, TASK_COST, CHAT_ALLOWANCE, the overflow hold (chatOverflowHold) and ONBOARD_EXTRA_BRAND_COST.src/components/home/HomeFaq.tsx:12–53

View pricing
visitor
/{lang}/pricing (also the Plans section #price on the home page and the #pricing section on industry pages)
Opens Pricing from the nav or footer, follows the Plans anchor on the home page, or scrolls to it.On /pricing: the headline 'Cheaper than one hire.' with 'Prices in Saudi riyals, excluding VAT. Cancel anytime.', and two badges: '3-day free trial: 50 credits and 30 chat messages — no card' and 'Pay yearly, get two months free (coming soon)'. Four plan cards: Starter 299 SAR/month with 300 credits, 'Unlimited brands, 1 seat' and 250 chat messages a month; Pro 499 with 700 credits, 3 seats and 650 chat messages (highlighted); Business 999 with 1,500 credits reset monthly, 7 seats and 1,300 chat messages, 'For bigger teams and businesses'; Agency 'Contact us', annual billing, 'Custom credits, from 1,500 a month', custom monthly chat messages and 'Seats sized to your team'. Then 'What does each task cost?': social post / ad / WhatsApp message 1; image 1–2; SEO article / lead qualification card / weekly plan 2; weekly report / unit economics 3; sales scripts / pricing review / cash forecast / growth experiments / follow-up cadence 4; go-to-market plan / board deck / strategy review 5; chatting with the team, up to the plan's monthly messages (then only if the owner turns on continuing on credits, at most 5 credits a message) Included; setting up the first brand from its URL Free; each added brand 9 (refunded if the brand file cannot be built; the manual form is free). The FAQ and a final CTA follow. On the home page, 'Plans.' repeats the 'Prices in Saudi riyals' line and the trial stamp, with Razan saying 'Before you spend… let me run the numbers.', then shows the same four cards in a short form (price, credits and the first three features, a 'Start here' tape on Pro) with the line 'Every plan has the same rule: nothing goes out without your approval.', and no credit table.A static render from the dictionary (i18n.ts pricing). The cards read PLAN_PRICE_SAR, PLAN_CREDITS, PLAN_SEATS, CHAT_ALLOWANCE and AGENCY_MIN (the Business price and credits) from src/lib/pricing.ts, and each card carries data-tier (starter, pro, business, agency). creditRows groups the table's task types by TASK_COST / TASK_COUNT, inserts the image price range from the media catalogue, then appends the chat row (with the overflow hold), the free first-brand row and the ONBOARD_EXTRA_BRAND_COST (9) row. The home Plans section maps PUBLIC_TIERS over the same plans. Nothing is read from the account or from billing.src/app/[lang]/pricing/page.tsx:20–35

Pick a plan
visitor
Plan card button in Pricing (and in the home Plans section)
Clicks the button on one of the four plan cards: 'Start free' on Starter, Pro and Business, or 'Contact us' on Agency.Starter, Pro and Business open /{lang}/app/signup with no plan parameter. Agency's 'Contact us' opens /{lang}/enterprise#contact, the Agencies page scrolled to its #contact block: the 'Not available yet, plainly' list and the 'Talk to us' contact form.Plain link picked by plan.id on the pricing page and on the home Plans cards: agency → /{lang}/enterprise#contact, anything else → /{lang}/app/signup. Whichever self-serve card was clicked, the account created at signup starts on plan 'trial' with the trial credits (50) and trialEnds set to now + 3 days. A paid plan is chosen later, inside the app.
  • Starter, Pro or Business → /{lang}/app/signup, no plan param
  • Agency 'Contact us' → /{lang}/enterprise#contact

visitor

Reads the explainer page and may click an 'Ask AI' button or a 'Read next' link.In order: 1. The Ask AI buttons are external links that carry the question in ?q=, naming this page on the canonical host (appHost). Nothing goes through Thakeen's servers.

20
Hit an error page
visitor
Any /{lang}/... page that throws while rendering
Opens a page whose render fails, then clicks 'Try again' or 'Home'.A bilingual 'صار خطأ غير متوقع' / 'Something went wrong' screen with 'إعادة المحاولة · Try again', which re-renders the route, and 'الرئيسية · Home' to /ar. A 'ref: <digest>' line shows when a digest exists. When the layout itself fails, a plain full-page version shows 'Try again' only.The route error boundary logs the digest, or the message, to the browser console. reset() retries the render. global-error.tsx replaces the whole document for errors thrown by the layout.
  • Try again → reset()
  • Home → /ar
  • layout error → global-error page
src/app/[lang]/error.tsx:12–35

22
Open a partner's link
visitor
/p/{CODE} (a partner's link, shared outside the product)
Opens a partner's link, /p/{CODE}.The browser is redirected (307) to /{lang}/app/signup, at the email step. With a known code the address carries ?partner=CODE and, on the password step the optional 'Partner code' box already holds CODE, with the line 'Partner code CODE: 15% off your first month.' under it With an unknown code, or over the limit, signup opens with nothing set.GET /p/{CODE}, public. The language is ?lang=, then the nashr_lang cookie, then Accept-Language, Arabic by default. The code is trimmed, upper-cased and checked for form; then 60 per IP per hour (plink:ip). partnerByCode reads the code to its partner in Redis. A known code sets the first-party httpOnly cookie nashr_partner for 30 days, value CODE.<click time>, so the 30-day attribution window runs from this click. A visitor who already carries a valid partner cookie keeps it (first touch): clicking again, or another partner's link, never restarts the 30 days, and the redirect carries the held code. The credits referral (?ref=) is a separate thing this never reads. A signed-in person is sent on to /{lang}/app by the signup page.
  • unknown or malformed code → signup with nothing set
  • over 60 per IP per hour → signup with nothing set
  • valid partner cookie already held → kept, redirect carries the held code
  • already signed in → /{lang}/app by the signup page

Run a free tool
visitor
/{lang}/tools/{tool} (ToolForm)
Fills the tool's fields, including a Language select on every tool except Hashtag Generator, and clicks 'Generate free'.The button changes to 'Noor is writing…'. On success the text appears in the output panel with a Copy button. NOW, while the model is paused, every submit shows 'The free tools are paused for now. Please check back later.' Other outcomes: - Over the limit: 'Daily limit of N reached. Sign up free for more.' with a 'Sign up' link. - A model error: 'Something failed, try again.'POST /api/tools/{slug}. The route: 1. Answers 404 unknown_tool for an unknown slug. 2. Checks modelState() before the rate limit. The state is paused when CLAUDE_PAUSED is true and ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is set, and also on a Vercel production deployment with no key. A paused call answers 503 'paused' without using any allowance. With no key elsewhere (a laptop, CI) the state is mock. 3. Reads currentUser(). A visitor gets 5 calls per IP (rate:tool:{ip}) and a signed-in account 50 (rate:tool:u:{email}), each in a 24-hour window that starts at the first use. Over that it answers 429 rate_limited with the limit. 4. Cuts each field to 600 characters and forces the language to one of the four choices (default: Saudi Arabic). 5. In mock, returns a '[DEV MOCK]' echo, after the allowance is counted. 6. Otherwise calls claude-sonnet-5 (adaptive thinking, low effort, 2000 max tokens) with a Noor system prompt, with one retry; both attempts are bounded by a 45-second budget inside the route's 60 seconds. On success it meters token usage as kind 'tool' (the global monthly total, plus the account when signed in) and increments toolusage:{slug}. No credits are charged and there is no trial check. An error in the call answers 502 'failed'.

Needs: None. A session raises the daily ceiling from 5 to 50.

  • paused (current) → 503 'paused' message, allowance not used
  • mock (no API key, not production) → '[DEV MOCK]' echo
  • 429 rate_limited (5 per IP anonymous, 50 per account signed in) → limit message with a Sign up link, even for a signed-in person
  • 502 failed → 'Something failed, try again.'
  • network failure or non-JSON reply: there is no try/catch, so the button stays on 'Noor is writing…'

Vote for a wish
any-signed-in
/{lang}/updates wishlist (WishVote)
Clicks Vote on an item, or clicks Voted again to take the vote back.The count and pressed state change right away, before the server answers. Clicks are ignored while one is in flight. On success the server's count replaces the shown one. - Session expired: the browser goes to login?next=/updates. - Over the limit: the change is undone and 'Too many votes in a short time. Try again shortly.' shows. - Any other failure: the change is undone and 'Could not save your vote. Please try again.' shows.POST /api/wishlist/vote {id, on}. The route checks currentUser() first (401 auth). It allows 60 per account per hour (rate:wishvote:{email}, 429 slow_down). It requires an id that is a visible seeded item or a promoted one (400 unknown_item). setVote then runs sadd or srem on wish:votes:{id} and wish:mine:{email} side by side and returns the new count. on states the wanted result, so repeating it changes nothing. Any role may vote. The vote is recorded under the account's email, so a member votes as the owner's account.

42
Create account
visitor
/{lang}/app/signup -> POST /api/auth/signup
Clicks "Create account".The button disables while waiting. On success there is no message: the browser goes to ?next= if one was given, else to /{lang}/app/start, with ?url= prefilled when a site of 4 or more characters was carried. Errors appear in red. "This email is already registered." also appears for an address that is already a team member's login. "Invalid email." also appears when the server refuses ':', '#' or more than 120 characters. "Password too short (8+ characters)." is only a server backstop, because the rules are checked first. "That partner code is not valid. Check it or leave the field empty." appears for a typed code that is not a partner's. On the rate limit the page says "Too many sign-ups from this connection. Try again in an hour." When Turnstile is on and the widget has no token, the form says "The security check wasn't completed. Complete it and try again." before sending; a refused check shows the same text and a Cloudflare outage shows "The security check isn't responding right now. Try again shortly." A dropped connection shows "Couldn't reach the server. Check your internet connection and try again." and a 5xx "Something went wrong on our side. Please try again shortly."The rate limit comes first: 5 per IP per hour, and every attempt counts, including refused ones (429 slow_down). Then the route normalises the email (trim, lowercase) and checks validEmail, strongPassword and an optional phone of at least 9 digits; the form sends no phone. When both Turnstile keys are set, the token is checked at Cloudflare before the address is looked up (400 captcha_failed, 503 captcha_unavailable); with either key missing nothing is checked. signupPartner then reads a typed partner code or the link cookie (a typed code that is not a partner's is 400 bad_partner_code). Under lock:login:{email}, getLogin answers 409 exists for an owner login or for a member login on its owner's roster. Otherwise createUser runs: bcrypt cost 10, plan trial, 50 credits, trialEnds = now + 3 days, sessionsAfter = now. It writes user:{email} and adds the email to users:all. Then registerCode writes ref:{CODE}, applyReferral runs when ref is valid, the partner attribution runs, and createSession stores a 32-byte hex token as session:{token} for 30 days, in the httpOnly nashr_session cookie. track(signup) records the event. There is no email verification, no welcome email and no model call, so CLAUDE_PAUSED does not affect this step. The 3-day trial clock starts at once.

Needs: Address not yet used by an owner login or by a member login on its owner's roster; Turnstile passed when it is on

  • 409 exists -> "This email is already registered."
  • 400 bad_email -> "Invalid email."
  • 400 weak_password -> "Password too short (8+ characters)."
  • 400 bad_partner_code -> partner code text
  • 400 captcha_failed / 503 captcha_unavailable -> security-check text
  • 429 -> "Too many sign-ups from this connection. Try again in an hour."
  • network or 5xx -> connection or server text
  • ok + next -> go to next
  • ok -> /{lang}/app/start[?url=site]

Referral bonus
system
/api/auth/signup (server)
Runs automatically when the new owner signed up through a referral link ?ref=CODE.Nothing is announced. The signup form ignores the returned `referred` flag, so the new owner only sees 100 credits instead of 50 in the sidebar. The referrer gets no notification; the count shows on their /{lang}/app/refer page.applyReferral resolves ref:{CODE} (upper-cased) to the referrer. It refuses unknown codes, the same email and a missing account. adjust() adds 50 credits to each of the two accounts under each account's user lock. lpush adds an entry to referrals:{referrer}, and referredBy:{new} is set.

Partner code
visitor
/{lang}/app/signup -> POST /api/auth/signup (server)
Arrives through a partner link /p/CODE (which fills the partner code box) or types a partner's code in the optional "Partner code" box, then creates the account.Nothing extra is announced on success: the hint under the box already said "A partner code gives you 15% off your first month." (or "Partner code CODE: 15% off your first month." when the link filled it). A typed code that is not a partner's stops the signup with "That partner code is not valid. Check it or leave the field empty." and no account is created. The partner themselves (an approved partner's address that signs up later) gets a Partner entry in the sidebar.signupPartner runs after the Turnstile check and before anything is created, so a script cannot probe which codes exist without passing it. A typed code wins over the link cookie: it must belong to a partner ("bad" gives 400 bad_partner_code); without one, the code in the nashr_partner cookie counts if it is still a partner's. After the account exists, afterSignup attributes it: referredBy = {partner, code, at, via, ref} (the 30 days run from the link click when the code is the link's, else from signup), firstMonthOffer = 15% for the first paid month, and the account is added to the partner's refs set. A partner's own address is never attributed to itself. The link cookie is deleted either way. A failed attribution is logged and never fails the signup. The response carries partner: true when the account was attributed. This is separate from the credits referral (?ref=), which gives no discount.

Needs: A typed code is a partner's code, or a valid partner link cookie is held; the account has no earlier referredBy

  • typed code not a partner's -> 400 bad_partner_code, nothing created
  • no typed code + valid link cookie -> attributed via link
  • typed code that is not the link's -> attributed via code
  • own address as the partner -> not attributed
  • attribution fails -> logged, signup still succeeds

Trial runs out
any-signed-in
TrialBanner; LimitModal dialog (generate, reports, chat, item image/video buttons, an added brand's setup on /start)
Keeps using the app after trialEnds (3 days after signup) and tries something that spends credits: generate, a report draft, a chat message, an item image or video, or setting up an added brand from its URL on /start. Before the 3 days end, a trial can also use up its 30 chat messages.The banner switches to "Your trial has ended". Spending actions come back as trial_over and open a dialog: "Your free trial has ended" / "Everything in your inbox is kept. Choose a plan and the team picks up where it stopped." The dialog has "View plans" (to billing) and "Maybe later". Admins and members get the ask-the-owner text with a single "OK". The generate panel also shows "Trial ended. Subscribe to continue.", and so does the start page when an added brand's setup is refused. Chat answers with a reply that says "Your free trial has ended, so the agents won't answer new messages until a plan is active", together with the dialog; the message is not saved and no model runs. Signing in and reading keep working, and the first brand's setup is free, so it is not refused here. While the model is paused, generate shows "Content generation is paused for now. No credits were used." because it checks the pause first; chat after the trial ended gives the trial-ended reply, not the paused one, because it checks the trial first (within the trial it gives its paused reply). The item image and video buttons, and an added brand's setup when the balance covers its 9 credits, still raise the trial dialog. The line under the chat box counts "N of 30 messages left in your trial"; when all 30 are used a dialog says "Your trial's chat messages are used up" and the message goes back into the box.trialOver(u) is true when the plan is trial and trialEnds has passed. /api/generate answers 503 model_paused first, then 402 trial_over before any charge. The media start (renderImage, renderVideo) answers 402 trial_over first. POST /api/onboard/confirm, which calls no model, answers 402 trial_over for an added brand whose setup is not already paid for, and the start page raises the dialog with limitFromError; nothing is charged. When the balance is below the 9 credits an added brand's setup costs (ONBOARD_EXTRA_BRAND_COST), the start page has already turned Go off and shows the balance note with a "View plans" button that opens the credits dialog, so no request is sent. POST /api/chat checks trialOver first: 402 trial_over with the trial-ended reply and limit "trial", before the short-window ceilings and before any message of the trial's allowance is counted; chat() repeats the check as a backstop. Then chatRefusal and reserveChatTurn count the trial's 30 messages (CHAT_ALLOWANCE.trial, once for the whole trial); past them the route answers 402 chat_allowance and ChatPanel raises the chat dialog. Chat tools push limit:trial, which ChatPanel turns into the dialog. The trial check stops applying when a founder extends the trial with extendTrial (1-90 days, counted from now if the trial has already ended) or when the plan moves off trial.

Needs: plan = trial and trialEnds in the past (or, for the chat dialog, all 30 trial chat messages used)

  • owner -> "View plans" -> billing
  • admin/member -> "OK"
  • model paused -> generate says paused; chat says trial ended first
  • chat after the trial -> trial-ended reply + dialog, nothing saved
  • 30 trial chat messages used -> chat dialog, message not sent
  • added brand setup on /start -> dialog + "Trial ended. Subscribe to continue."
  • added brand setup with fewer than 9 credits -> Go off, balance note, no request
  • founder extends trial -> banner shows days again

Log in
visitor

Then createSession(login email) for 30 days and track(login) under the owner's email. There is no lockout beyond the rate limits.

Request reset link
visitor
/{lang}/app/forgot -> POST /api/auth/forgot
Types their email and clicks "Send the link".The form is replaced by a status box. With email delivery configured, it says "If this email has an account, a link arrives within minutes. Check spam too.", the same text for unknown addresses. Without it, it says "Email delivery is not enabled on Thakeen yet, so no link can reach you. Contact support and we reset your password manually.", plus a "Contact support on WhatsApp" button when SUPPORT_WHATSAPP is set. A malformed address shows "Invalid email". Complete it and try again."; a Cloudflare outage shows the "isn't responding" text.Format check first (400 bad_email). The per-IP limit (8 per 15 min) still answers ok, silently, and comes before the captcha call so a script cannot make Cloudflare calls without limit. When both Turnstile keys are set, the token is then checked (400 captcha_failed, 503 captcha_unavailable). The per-address limit (3 per 15 min) also answers ok silently. getLogin runs, and a member counts only while its owner exists. When a login exists and mailReady() is true (RESEND_API_KEY and MAIL_FROM), the route makes a 64-hex token and stores pwreset:{sha256} = {email, kind} for 1 hour. It emails "Reset your Thakeen password" with appOrigin()/{lang}/app/reset?token=… to the login's own address. A provider refusal or a send slower than 10 seconds is logged to admin:log as mail:refused, without the recipient, while the page still says a link is on its way.

  • rate limited -> same answer, no email

Set new password
visitor
/{lang}/app/reset -> POST /api/auth/reset
Types a new password and clicks "Save and log in".On success the browser goes to /{lang}/app, signed in. The main error is "This link is invalid or expired. Request a new one." with a "Reset your password" link to /{lang}/app/forgot. Other errors: "The password does not meet the rules." (only a backstop), "Too many attempts. For a member, a suspended owner gets 403. Only the member's own record and its own sessionsAfter change, then a new session is created, unless the member is seat-paused (403 disabled).

A signed-out visitor sees "Checking your invite…". The token disappears from the address bar.The link is built with appOrigin() in the invite's language, with the token in the URL fragment, which never reaches a server. With mail on, the invite is created, the Thakeen-branded mail "You are invited to join a team on Thakeen" is sent, and if the send fails the invite is discarded and the inviter gets 502 send_failed, so nobody holds a link that did not arrive. JoinForm reads the fragment (64 hex, lower-cased) and keeps it in sessionStorage for this tab. It strips the fragment with history.replaceState. /join is a public path, and the trial banner is hidden there.

Needs: Public; the invite lasts 7 days

  • signed in -> log-out-first screen
  • no or malformed token -> incomplete-link message

Accept invite
visitor
/{lang}/app/join -> POST /api/team/accept {token, name, password, lang}
Types a name and a password and clicks "Join". The email is fixed by the invite and read-only."Join" stays focusable but marked disabled until a name is typed and the three password rules pass; pressing it with a name but unmet rules names them and focuses the password box. While waiting it shows "Joining…". On success the browser goes to /{lang}/app inside the inviting account, with an Admin or Member chip and the account's shared credits. If that account has no brand yet, it goes on to /start: an admin gets the first-login game, a member the wait note. Errors: "This account has no free seat right now. Tell whoever invited you.", "This email already has a login. Log in with it, or ask for an invite to another address.", "Too many attempts. Try again in a few minutes.", or a return to the invalid-invite screen. "Enter your name." and "The password does not meet the rules." are server backstops.Rate limit: 10 per IP per 15 min. acceptInvite needs a live invite. It cleans the name (control characters removed, 80 chars), checks strongPassword and hashes with bcrypt before taking any lock. A 30 s inviteuse:{hash} lock stops two accepts of one invite running at once. Under lock:team it re-reads the invite and its index entry, the owner, the inviter and the seat count against seatLimit (409 seats): the founder's seat count when set, else Trial 3, Starter 1, Pro 3, Business 7 and Agency 7, owner included. Under lock:login the address must be free across user: and member: (409 unavailable). It writes member:{email} with acceptedAt = sessionsAfter = now and adds it to the roster. It then deletes the invite, which is what makes it single use. The route runs destroySession and createSession(member), and puts a bell item "<name> joined your members" on the owner. There are no trial credits, no referral code and no signup tracking.

Needs: Signed out, live invite, a free seat, address not yet a login

  • 400 weak_password

  • 409 unavailable

  • ok -> /{lang}/app

60
Session ends
system
Any /{lang}/app page or API call
Happens automatically when a session stops being valid. For everyone: after 30 days (2 hours for an impersonation session, which also ends with its console key session, after 15 minutes without a request, or at a key rotation), a password change or reset on another device, a founder suspension, a founder password reset, or account deletion. For a member also: removal, leaving, a seat pause, or the owner's password reset.The next page load inside /app redirects to /{lang}/app/login, with no return path kept. API calls answer 401, and each screen shows its own error. There is no advance warning. Trying to log in again then shows the suspended text for a suspended account, "Wrong email or password." for a removed member, or the seat-paused text ("Your login is paused because the account's plan has no free seat. Ask the account owner to upgrade.") for a seat-paused member.currentUser() returns null in these cases: the cookie or session:{token} is gone (Redis TTL); a key-bound impersonation session whose console key session is no longer live (keyImpLive); the owner is suspended; the session predates sessionsAfter. For a member also: the record is missing or disabled, it is off the roster, the session predates member.sessionsAfter or owner.teamSessionsAfter, or the owner is gone or suspended. A founder reset sets sessionsAfter and teamSessionsAfter and revokes MCP; a founder suspension sets suspended.
  • expired -> login
  • revoked by password change/reset -> login
  • key-bound impersonation ended -> login
  • suspended -> login refuses with suspended text
  • member removed -> login says wrong email or password; seat-paused -> seat-paused text

Noor starts the build
system
/{lang}/app/chat (ChatPanel first run)
The page itself posts {url: confirmed URL} to POST /api/onboard, once per page load (ranOnboard ref). The owner clicks nothing.Progress card 'Noor is building your workspace', with 'Reading your site, writing your brand file and a starter plan. Stay on this page.' and a bar. The Send button is disabled while this runs, and a typed message is not sent. For an added brand the 9 credits are taken before the stream starts; the credits pill updates when the build ends.Checks in order: session (401 text); forbid 'onboard' (403 forbidden); URL of 4+ characters (400 bad_url); brand exists (409 already_onboarded); URL differs from onboardok:{scope} (403 unconfirmed); model paused (503 paused). Then, for an added brand (cost = onboardCost(u.brandId) = 9) with no held charge, cannotPay answers 402 JSON {error:'trial_over'|'agent_cap'|'no_credits', need, credits} from the session record (trial, Noor's monthly cap in that brand from governance, balance), before the daily allowance; a cap or balance refusal also adds a 'cap_reached' or 'no_credits' row to that brand's activity log. Then rate allow('onboard:{account email}', 3, 86400), a fixed 24-hour window (429 slow_down). Then an owned lock onboardlock:{scope} for 330 s (maxDuration 300 + 30), taken with db.lockOwned and released only with its token (409 busy). Under the lock it reads the brand, the confirmation and the Dictionary answers again (409 already_onboarded / 403 unconfirmed). For an added brand chargeOnboard then debits 9 credits to agent 'noor' in that brand scope, under Noor's cap, and writes the hold User.onboardPaid[scope] in the same locked save (402 if the ledger refuses). A run that finds a hold reuses it without charging and refreshes its time. On a fresh charge an 'onboard_charged' activity row is written and warnAgentCap runs (a notification when Noor crosses 80% of her cap). Then it opens an SSE stream with a 10 s ping. The first brand is free: no charge and no trial check. The whole request shares one deadline.

Needs: Admin or owner, no brand, confirmed URL, model on, fewer than 3 tries in the account's current 24-hour window; for an added brand with no held charge also an active trial or plan, Noor under her cap and 9+ credits

  • 503 paused (current production state) -> onboard-paused-notice, nothing charged
  • 402 trial_over / agent_cap / no_credits (added brand) -> limit dialog + 'Setup of this brand did not start' (onboard-build-fail)
  • 429 slow_down -> 'You reached today's brand setup limit' (onboard-build-fail)
  • 409 busy or already_onboarded -> 'Setup is still running' text
  • 403 unconfirmed / 403 forbidden / 400 / 401 -> generic failure text
  • held charge from an unfinished run -> reused, not charged again
  • 200 -> live progress

The SSRF guard allows http(s) only and blocks credentials, localhost/.local/.internal, and private, loopback, link-local, metadata and multicast IPs. It re-checks every redirect hop (at most 5). The crawl has a 9 s timeout and no JS rendering. It keeps the first 600,000 characters of HTML and extracts title, description, text, links, platform (Salla, Zid, Shopify, WooCommerce, WordPress, Webflow, Wix), inline hex colours and fonts. 72
Setup failed, refresh
owner
/{lang}/app/chat (Getting started thread)
Hits a refusal or a failure during the build, then refreshes.A 402 (an added brand the trial, Noor's cap or the balance cannot cover) raises the matching dialog ('Your free trial has ended', 'This agent reached its monthly cap' or 'You're out of credits') and Noor says 'Setup of this brand did not start, and no credits were used.' A 429 says 'You reached today's brand setup limit. This try charged nothing, and a setup already paid for stays paid. Try again tomorrow.' Any 409 (busy: a second tab or a reload during a run; or already_onboarded) shows 'Setup is still running. Refresh in a minute.' Any other refusal (403 unconfirmed or forbidden, 400, 401) shows '⚠︎ Could not set up the workspace. Refresh to try again.' A stream error on a paid added brand before the brand file was saved shows, once the refund went through, '⚠︎ Could not set up this brand, and the 9 credits went back to your balance ({credits} credits). Refreshing tries again and uses 9 credits.' and the credits pill updates. If that refund itself failed, the generic text shows and the charge stays held, so the next run is not charged again. A stream error after the brand file was saved (any brand) shows '⚠︎ Your brand file was saved, but the rest of the setup did not finish. Refresh to open the workspace, then check your files.' Other stream errors (model failure after one retry, or the request's deadline) and a dropped connection show the generic text. The progress card disappears.Refusals (402, 429, 409, 403, 400, 401) come back as plain answers before any stream, and nothing is charged. The 409 busy, the 409 already_onboarded and 403 unconfirmed from the re-read under the lock, and a 402 from the ledger itself come after the daily count; the ones taken under the lock release it. On a stream error the route logs and tracks 'error'. If it holds a charge and the brand was not saved, refundOnboard gives the 9 credits back, lowers Noor's counter and drops the hold in one save, at most once (null when another run already refunded), and an 'onboard_refunded' activity row records it. The model calls end by the request's own deadline, so a slow model throws and the refund runs before the platform's kill. If the refund itself fails the hold stays: the next run reuses it, or the daily cron refunds a hold nobody used for a day (sweepOnboardHolds, only dropped when the brand exists). If the brand was saved, settleOnboard drops the hold with no refund. The error event carries refunded, credits and saved, and the owned lock is released in finally. Messages already pushed (intro, unreachable note) stay in the thread. With no brand saved, onboardok is not cleared, so until it expires (a day after Go) a refresh runs the build again (and charges an added brand again after a refund). Each try that passes the paused and payment checks counts toward the 3-per-24-hours allowance, taken before the lock, so a reload during a run also uses a try. If the browser disconnects, the server keeps working (send() swallows the closed stream), so the thread is complete on reload.

Needs: Admin or owner, no brand

  • refresh -> onboard-build-start again (an added brand pays 9 again after a refund)
  • 402 -> top up, subscribe or raise Noor's cap in Governance; the manual form and the no-website path are free
  • 429 -> wait until 24 hours after the first try; the manual form is the way in
  • 409 already_onboarded (another tab finished first) -> same 'still running' text; refresh opens the workspace
  • error after the brand file was saved -> 'brand file was saved' text, no refund; refresh opens the workspace
  • refund failed or run killed by the platform -> hold kept; the next run reuses it or the daily cron refunds it after a day
  • connection dropped mid-build -> generic text, but a refresh after the server finishes opens the built workspace

An ended trial gets 402 trial_over with limit 'trial' and a message, before anything is counted (rule 330). chatRefusal then paces the account: 30 turns an hour and 150 a day on a paid plan, 10 and 30 on a trial; over it 429 slow_down with retry 'hour' or 'day' (rule 331). reserveChatTurn counts the message against the plan's monthly chat allowance; past it 402 chat_allowance (paused) or chat_credits (continue on credits is on but the balance is too low). Only then does chat() save the user message to chat:{scope}. While paused, PAUSED_REPLY is saved, no tool runs, nothing is charged and the reserved message is given back. When unpaused, the chat turn and its tools can charge credits and raise the credits, trial or agent-cap limit dialog (chat/generation stage).

Open the chat
any-signed-in
/{lang}/app/chat
Opens the chat from the sidebar 'main' row (or the Noor row), at the end of onboarding, or through a link with ?conv=, ?agent= or ?prompt=.The header shows the agent's initial and name with the thread title (e.g. 'Noor · Getting started'), a '+ New conversation' button and a credits pill that links to billing. Below it: the agent tabs and the thread's 40 newest messages, including any Majed handoff card, Reem away card or brand-change card with its stored state. Under the input a small line shows the month's chat allowance ('{left} of {allowance} messages left this month'). On a thread with at most one message, a 'From the team today' card can appear. It shows 'Waiting for you (n) · Review all', the first pending item as a card, and lines 2-4 of the latest brief. An empty thread shows Noor's greeting, or the specialist's one-line description, with that agent's suggestion chips. On trial accounts: if onboarding stored a goal, a bar shows the goal, the first plan task that is not yet added, and 'Activate Noor' (links to billing). Without a goal, a 'First up' hint shows instead (import catalog, add offer, connect, first batch). On xl screens a side panel shows metrics, channels, the routine feed, the first unread notification and files, all as links.The layout and the page call currentUser(). With a brand file, the page reads 11 things in parallel: history(scope, conv) with brand-card states, connections, schedule, files, latest brief, notifications, brand, the light task index since kpiSince() (90 days), onboardplan:{scope}, the launch journey and chatStatus(u), the account's chat allowance. It then reads, for every handoff card named in the thread, the handoff records and the settings in one batch (a record that is gone is sent as null), computes KPIs, loads the first pending task, and sets firstStep: catalog if there is no products.md, else offer if the brand has no offers, else connect if nothing is connected, else batch. ?conv must be 6-16 hex characters. ?agent must be a known agent other than Noor. ?prompt pre-fills the input (up to 2000 characters) without sending it. The page passes the login's role flags (publish, brand.write, generate, drafts) to ChatPanel, which hides the buttons a role may not use; the routes check the same actions. In the browser, GET /api/conversations loads the thread list; with ?conv it sets the agent from that conversation record (Noor if not found).

Needs: Signed in, any role; the active brand has a brand file

  • No session: redirect to /{lang}/app/login
  • No brand file and not admin/owner ('onboard'): redirect to /{lang}/app/start
  • No brand file, admin or owner, no confirmed site: redirect to /{lang}/app/start (with ?url= only when the link carried ?onboard=)
  • No brand file, admin or owner, site confirmed: the page reads the history and the chat status and the build runs inside the Getting-started thread (the first-run effect; an added brand's setup that cannot be paid opens the limit dialog; while paused the build answers 'paused' before its pay check)
  • ?prompt= only pre-fills the input; nothing is sent
  • Side panel hidden below the xl breakpoint

98
Send a message
any-signed-in
/{lang}/app/chat (ChatPanel input, suggestion chips)
Types in 'Message Noor… e.g. prepare 10 posts for National Day' and presses Send or Enter, or taps a suggestion chip. The '/' key focuses the input.The person's bubble appears at once, the input clears and Send is disabled. A working line shows 'Noor is thinking…' (or '{Agent}…'), then status lines prefixed with the agent's name: first '{Agent}: Reading your brand file and my notes…', then one line per tool. The reply appears as a bubble with the agent's name and rendered markdown. Under it can come: item cards, a Majed handoff card, brand-change cards, a Reem away card, and short labels ('{n} added to approvals', 'Brand file updated', 'A brand change waits for your Save', 'An absence waits for your Save', 'Your to-do list was updated'). Each reply gets thumbs, the credits pill updates, and the 'messages left' line under the box counts down. In a new thread the title becomes the first 60 characters of the message. WHILE PAUSED: after the first status line the reply reads 'The agents are paused for now, so I can't reply or create content yet. No credits were used.' (Arabic for Arabic readers), with no cards.The browser sends POST /api/chat {text, conv, agent, stream:true} with the x-lang header. The server checks the session and trims the text to 4000 characters (empty text gives 400). An unknown agent falls back to Noor. Before anything is saved it runs the refusals in a fixed order (trial_over, slow_down, then the month's allowance; see the next two steps) and reserves one message with reserveChatTurn. It then streams SSE with a ping every 10 s (or answers JSON when stream is off). chat() sends the first status, then saves the user message to chat:{scope}[:{conv}] (the thread keeps 400) and checks modelState(). When the model is on, it reads 10 things in parallel: brand, this agent's preferences (up to 30), memory files, file names, connections, the thread, feedbackFor with chat thumbs, the handoff notes left for this agent (its last 5 from 7 days, added to its prompt as data), who is acting, and the brand's working-days calendar. The thread sent to the model is at most the last 16 messages and 24,000 characters. It then runs up to 5 claude-opus-5 calls with tools, and meter() records each call. Before every call the per-message ceiling CHAT_TURN_MAX_USD ($0.35) is checked in code: a call's output is capped to what is left, and the loop ends when too little is left for a useful answer. The system prompt quotes TASK_COST (weekly_plan=2, weekly_report=3, followup_seq=4), the HELP answers, and for planning questions: plans differ in monthly credits and seats, brands are unlimited on every plan, the first brand's setup is free, each added brand's setup from its URL costs ONBOARD_EXTRA_BRAND_COST (9) credits (the manual form is free), and the trial is 3 days with PLAN_CREDITS.trial (50) credits. The reply is saved with at most 10 item cards, 5 brand cards and 5 away cards, plus one handoff card for Majed. The route then settles the reserved message, calls track('chat') and, for a non-default thread, touchConversation (count +1, updatedAt, title if still '…'). Chat itself costs no credits inside the allowance; only tools charge. WHILE PAUSED: no model call and no tool run. PAUSED_REPLY in the reader's language is saved and returned; the message is given back to the allowance (no model call was made); the conversation count still goes up.

Needs: Signed in, any role; not busy with another reply; trial not over; a chat turn left (plan allowance, or credits when continue-on-credits is on)

  • Paused (CLAUDE_PAUSED = true): fixed paused reply, no tool, no charge; both messages saved; the allowance count is given back
  • Refused before saving (trial over, too many in a short time, month used up, too few credits for a message on credits): see the next steps; nothing is saved on the server
  • Server error: SSE error chat_failed gives '⚠︎ Could not reply. Try again.' and the reserved message is given back
  • Connection drops: 'Connection dropped, recovering the reply…', then GET /api/chat?conv= every 5 s up to 40 times. Found: the reply shows. Not found: '⚠︎ Connection lost. Try again.'
  • The loop ends without a closing answer: model refusal or 5 calls used gives 'Done. Check your approvals inbox.'; the $0.35 ceiling reached gives 'This reply reached its limit, so I stopped here…'; a reply cut off at its cap keeps its text with '(The reply was cut off at its limit…)'; out of time gives 'This reply ran out of time before I could finish it…'. The item, handoff, brand and away cards and the limit dialog are kept in every case
  • Workspace too large for even one call: 'I could not answer this message: your workspace files are too large for one message…'; no model call, so the message is not counted
  • No ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (dev/CI): '[DEV MOCK]' reply

Chat allowance counter
any-signed-in
/{lang}/app/chat (line under the message box)
Sends messages and watches the line under the box.Under the input a line reads '{left} of {allowance} messages left this month' (on a trial: '… left in your trial'). It turns to the warning colour at 10% or less left. When the month's messages are used it reads 'This month's chat messages ({allowance}) are used. Chat resumes on the 1st (UTC).' (a trial: chat comes back with a subscription). With 'continue on credits' on, it says each message is billed what it actually cost, at most {hold} credits, and needs at least {hold} credits in the balance; below that it says chat is paused. An allowance of 0 reads 'Chat is not available on this account. Contact support.'The allowance is the account's, not the login's: trial 30 once, starter 250, pro 650, business 1300 a UTC month, counted across seats, brands and MCP (CHAT_ALLOWANCE, chatAllowanceOf). The page and every chat answer carry chatStatus. For each turn reserveChatTurn adds one with INCRBY, so parallel turns can never pass the allowance. Past it the reservation is taken back, and a paid account whose owner turned on 'continue on credits' may go on when credits cover the hold, hold = ceil(CHAT_TURN_MAX_USD 0.35 / the dearest credit's cost) (chatOverflowHold), 5 credits today. After the turn settleChatTurn gives the message back when no model call was made (paused, failed), and on credits bills the metered cost at the floor rate, at most the hold. Chat inside the allowance never touches credits.

Needs: Signed in; any role (the count is the account's)

  • Allowance 0 (chat turned off for the account): no message goes through, not even on credits
  • Continue on credits is paid plans only; a trial has none
  • A turn that fails or makes no model call is given back to the count

The browser maps 402 chat_allowance, 402 chat_credits and 429 slow_down with refusalOf and 'unsends' the message, restoring the box and the credits pill.

  • Month's allowance used, continue on credits off: 402 chat_allowance, dialog
  • Continue on credits on, balance below the hold: 402 chat_credits, dialog
  • Allowance 0: 402 chat_allowance and the dialog says chat is off for the account

101
Ask for a full batch
any-signed-in
/{lang}/app/chat (generate_content tool)
Asks an agent for a full batch, e.g. '10 posts for National Day', a weekly plan, a sales script or a PRD.Status line '{Agent}: Writing the batch and running the second-pass reviewer…'. The system prompt tells the agent to sum up the angles in 2-3 lines and end with one offer (for 3 items or fewer, the tool also hands it the bodies to show). Up to 10 item cards appear, each with channel, title, rendered body and a 'Waiting' or 'Approved' badge. The label reads '{n} added to approvals', and the credits pill drops by the batch cost. When a limit stops the batch, the agent says so and the limit dialog opens. When Majed is asked for another agent's type (for example a Razan pricing review), the tool refuses and he proposes a handoff instead; every other agent may still write any type.runTool first applies the role gate for the tool, then generate_content checks the type with isTaskType and with leadRefusal (code, not prompt): in a turn where Majed is talking, a type another agent owns is refused with 'Not done: {type} is {agent}'s work…' and the way forward (propose_handoff); every other agent keeps calling any type. It then calls the one paid-text pipeline, generateBatch: brand file exists, trial not over, and, for cart_recovery only, that synced cart data exists (no_brand, trial_over, missing_data). renewIfDue runs, then charge(account email, TASK_COST[type], {scope, agent, cap}) under the user lock. It refuses with agent_cap (checked first) or no_credits without touching the balance; otherwise the plan's credits are spent first and top-up packs after them, and the agent's monthly counter is raised in the same save. warnAgentCap then runs. Inside a refunding try, it reads the agent's preferences, memory, workspace stats and feedback, and calls generate(): claude-opus-5, then the claude-sonnet-5 reviewer unless governance turned the reviewer off, then, for some types, a cross-agent review (Razan, Majed) that adds notes only. It strips dashes when governance's noEmDash is on, and applies capDiscounts for cart_recovery. On a throw, refund() returns the credits and lowers the agent counter. The items are saved in parallel as tasks with source 'chat' and a brief (notes, trimmed to 500 characters). A task is 'approved' only when auto-approve is on for the type, gateTask finds no banned word or placeholder, and no reviewer flagged it; otherwise it is 'pending'. Activity rows are recorded and judgeLater is queued.

Needs: Brand file; any role, members included (no role gate on this tool); credits >= TASK_COST[type] (e.g. social 10, ads 5, sales_script 4, followup_seq 4, whatsapp 3, gbp 3, cart_recovery 3, weekly_report 3, article 2, weekly_plan 2); agent cap not passed; trial not over; when Majed is talking, the type is his own

  • Unknown type: nothing charged; the agent is told the valid types
  • Majed asks for another agent's type (leadRefusal): refused in code, nothing charged; he is told who owns it and to use propose_handoff
  • No brand file: the agent asks the owner to run onboarding
  • Trial over: nothing charged; limit dialog 'Your free trial has ended'
  • Not enough credits: nothing charged; limit dialog 'You're out of credits'
  • Agent's monthly cap reached: nothing charged; limit dialog 'This agent reached its monthly cap'
  • cart_recovery with no synced cart data: nothing charged; the agent says to connect Salla or Shopify and press Sync on Reports
  • Generation fails: credits refunded; the agent relays the tool error
  • Auto-approve on and no brand rule broken and no reviewer flag: cards arrive Approved
  • A reviewer flagged an item: it stays Waiting even under auto-approve
  • Paused: never reached, because chat answers first. As a backstop, generate() throws ModelPaused and the charge is refunded.

Ask for 1-3 drafts
any-signed-in
/{lang}/app/chat (draft_items tool)
Asks for a few items, e.g. 'one caption', 'two WhatsApp messages', 'three ad variants', 'one Google post'.Status line '{Agent}: Drafting the items and reviewing them…'. The system prompt tells the agent to give one line per item's angle, say the items are also in the inbox, and add at most one sentence of advice and one offer. 1-3 item cards appear with the label '{n} added to approvals', and the credits pill drops by 1 per item. Only these four types are drafts: the tool's strict schema lists only them, and a draft_items call with any other type creates nothing and charges nothing; the tool result tells the agent to use generate_content, which charges that type's full batch cost (TASK_COST).runTool draft_items first applies isDraftType and then leadRefusal (Majed only): only DRAFT_TYPES (social, whatsapp, ads, gbp) pass, because the type is model input (a code backstop: the strict tool schema's enum already lists only these). Any other type returns 'Not a draft type. draft_items takes: social, whatsapp, ads, gbp; use generate_content for other types. Nothing was created and no credits were used.' before any read or charge. The tool schema's enum is built from the same DRAFT_TYPES. It clamps the count to 1-3. The cost is max(1, ceil(TASK_COST/TASK_COUNT × count)), which is 1 credit per item for all four types (social 10/10, whatsapp 3/3, ads 5/5, gbp 3/3). It then calls generateBatch with that count: brand file and trial (the cart-data check never applies, since cart_recovery is not a draft type), then agent cap and balance inside charge(), then warnAgentCap, generate() with count, refund on a throw, and tasks saved with source 'chat' and the brief. judgeLater is queued with requested = count.

Needs: Brand file; any role; a draft type (social, whatsapp, ads, gbp); enough credits; trial not over; agent cap not passed

  • Type outside social, whatsapp, ads and gbp: refused in code before any read or charge; nothing created; the tool result points the agent to generate_content
  • Majed asks for another agent's type: refused in code (leadRefusal); he is told to use propose_handoff
  • No brand file: the agent asks the owner to run onboarding
  • Trial over, no credits or agent cap: nothing charged; limit dialog
  • Generation fails: refunded; the agent relays the error
  • Paused: never reached; the paused reply instead

  • Owner, agent cap: 'Open Governance' goes to /{lang}/app/governance

  • Paused: only the /start setup check and its button, and the chat's trial-over and chat-message refusals, open it; the chat tools, the Generate page, handoff Run and the Getting-started build answer 'paused' first

  • Field not editable: refused, the agent lists the editable fields
  • Banned list would pass 400 characters: not saved
  • Brand changed since the card was made: 409 stale, nothing applied
  • Card older than 7 days: gone
  • Paused: the paused reply, no tool runs

Run a handoff item
any-signed-in
HandoffCard in chat
Presses Run on one item, or 'Run all'.The item shows 'Working on the item…' (the card reads the stored state every 10 s while it runs), then 'Done' with the item cards the batch made and the credits pill dropping by the item's cost. 'Run all' runs the items one at a time in order and stops at the first refusal. On a refusal the card shows its reason ('Another item of this handoff is running now…', 'No abandoned-cart data yet…', 'The generation failed and the credits were returned…') and the credits and cap limits open the limit dialog. If the answer never arrives, the card says the item may still be running and may be charged, and reads its state again by itself instead of offering Run.POST /api/handoffs/{id}/run {item} needs 'generate'; 60 runs an hour per account (429 slow_down); the id must be well formed; the item is an integer position, never anything else from the body. The paused model answers 503 model_paused before any charge. runHandoffItem reads the record only under sc(u), under the handoff's run lock (300 s, so a second run or a decline meanwhile is 409 busy), refuses an item that is done, declined or running (409), and sends the batch through generateBatch with the item's own type and agent, so the same checks run as for generate_content: brand, trial, missing_data, agent cap, credits. A failed generation is refunded and answered 502 generation_failed with the card's new state.

Needs: Owner, admin or any role with 'generate'; the item is still proposed or failed

  • No 'generate' right: Run is hidden, and the route answers 403
  • Too many requests in an hour: 429 slow_down
  • Another item running, done or declined: 409
  • Paused: 503 model_paused, no credits used
  • Trial over, no credits or agent cap: 402 and the limit dialog
  • cart_recovery without cart data: 409 missing_data, nothing charged
  • Not enough time left in the request: 503 too_late, nothing charged
  • Generation failed: refunded, 502; the item can be tried again

It also counts against the weekly cap and a hold, and the number of tries is bounded. The result is 'ok:N', a refusal (agent_cap, no_credits, trial_over), 'failed' (refunded; the item waits for the owner) or 'retry:paused' (paused: the item stays as it is). The notice is sent once per item.

Needs: Owner turned on auto-run and set a cap; a handoff Majed proposed to the owner in the last 7 days

  • Auto-run off, or no cap for the item's agent: nothing starts
  • Owner declined the item (Governance queue or the card): never starts
  • Paused: 'retry:paused', nothing charged, the next day's run asks again
  • Item type article or followup_seq: never starts on its own
  • Failed: refunded, the item waits for the owner's Run

Auto-approve on create
system
Creation sites (Generate, chat, weekly plan, routines, Majed's handoffs, MCP generate)
An agent creates an item of a type the owner set to auto-approve in Governance.The item appears directly under Approved, not Waiting, and does not count in the pending badge. An item that breaks a brand rule still arrives in Waiting, held with the rule named.Every batch builds its tasks in taskFromItem (src/lib/generate-batch.ts), which calls initialStatus(forcePending or flagged ? false : gov.autoApprove[type], brand, item). It returns 'approved' only when auto-approve is on, the batch was not forced to pending, no other agent flagged the item, and gateTask finds nothing. saveTask keeps the pending set in step. Auto-approval is not a human decision, so no decision record is written. decidedAt stays empty, so the judge can still send the item back. The chat publish_item tool also refuses it, and the owner publishes it from the inbox instead. Drafts saved through MCP save_draft are always created pending (initialStatus(false, ...)), even for an auto-approved type.

Needs: The owner switched auto-approve on for that type in Governance (owner only).

  • breaks a brand rule -> created pending
  • another agent flagged it -> created pending
  • MCP save_draft -> always pending

145
Judge scores the item
system
Background after generation; bell notifications
A batch was just created by chat, Generate, the weekly plan, routines, Majed's handoffs or MCP.A 'Quality NN' chip appears on each card at the next load. If any item scored below 70, a bell notification reads 'N of M <agent> item(s) scored below 70', with the judge's note for the first weak item. The notification links to the inbox. While paused none of this happens, because no batch is generated.generateBatch calls judgeLater after the batch is saved and the response is on its way. Claude Sonnet scores completeness, brand, honesty, language and format with the rubric kept in code (JUDGE_RUBRIC), and the score is weighted. An automatic-fail code (invented_fact, results_promise, fake_proof, fake_scarcity, injected_instruction) caps the score at 60 and honesty at 40, enforced in code. An item with no usable verdict stays unscored. Scores are written under the task lock, usage is metered as 'judge', and the batch's mean score and any automatic-fail codes go to the activity log. Each attempt is time-limited with one retry. While paused, scoreItems returns [] before making any call, and /api/generate refuses with 503 model_paused before charging.

Needs: Model on (not paused).

  • score < 70 -> quality notification
  • paused -> no score, no alert

See brand guard hold
any-signed-in
/{lang}/app (Inbox card)
Sees a card whose text breaks a brand rule.A red 'Held by brand guard' box names the rule ('Banned-words rule' or 'Empty-placeholder rule'). It explains that the item cannot be approved or published until edited, and lists each offending term with its excerpt highlighted. Its buttons are 'Fix the text', which opens the editor, and for banned words 'Brand file', which goes to /{lang}/app/onboarding. The Approve and Publish buttons are disabled.gateTask runs in the browser. Banned words are matched with Arabic letters folded and Arabic prefixes allowed. Unfilled placeholders such as [رقم], [date] or {{1}} are caught. At approval, personalisation slots ({{1}}, [name], [الاسم]) are allowed on WhatsApp, email, SMS and call channels, and script directions such as [VO] or [CTA] are allowed. The server applies the same rules on approve (409). It applies a stricter version on publish (409), where no placeholder or direction of any kind passes.

Needs: Customer-facing type (social, article, whatsapp, ads, gbp, followup_seq, cart_recovery).

  • Fix the text -> editor
  • Brand file -> /{lang}/app/onboarding

Approve an item
owner
/{lang}/app (Inbox card)
Clicks 'Approve' on a Waiting or Rejected card.The card flips to Approved at once (optimistic update) and leaves the Waiting filter. After the server confirms, the sidebar badge recounts and the journey card ticks 'approve'. On a brand violation, the card returns to its previous state and shows the held box. A refused role shows 'Your role does not allow this.' A failed or missing request shows 'The change was not saved. Check your connection and try again.'The browser sends PATCH /api/tasks/{id} {status:'approved'}. The route checks the session (401) and the 'drafts' role. Inside updateTask(sc(u), id), it requires 'publish' because the item ends approved (403, nothing saved). It sets decidedAt and runs gateTask on the final text (409 brand_violation, nothing saved). A task outside the caller's scope returns 404. Who decided is worked out from the session, never from the body. After the response it runs track('approve') and appends an 'approve' decision, with who decided, the judge's score and autofail code, to decisions:{scope}, plus one activity row. No credits and no model call, so it works while paused and after the trial ends.

Needs: Owner or admin; the item is not held by the brand guard.

  • 409 brand_violation -> held box
  • 403 forbidden -> role message
  • network/404 -> not saved message

/{lang}/app (Inbox editor)

158
Decisions recorded
system
Background (decisions:{scope}, prefs:{scope}, preferences.md)
Runs after every approve, reject, edit or reopen, and after an inbox publish that approved a pending item.Nothing on screen. Later drafts from the agents follow the owner's edits and reasons while 'Learn from your edits' is on in Governance (on by default).Each decision record is appended to decisions:{scope}, keeping the newest 500, whatever the learning setting. A record holds the task, the agent from the item type, type, channel, action, time, title, note, editPct, who decided (taken from the session), and the judge's score and autofail code. Each record except a publish also adds a 'decision' row to the activity log; the publish route writes its own 'published' row for every post. Preference notes are written only while learning is on, to prefs:{scope} (newest 100) and preferences.md, and are read per agent into prompts. Routines read the decisions, and the per-agent feedback reads them only when learning is on. Auto-approvals and publishes of already-approved items are not recorded.
  • learning off -> records only, no preference notes

Daily inbox reminders
system
Bell notifications and the daily e-mail
The daily cron runs at 05:00 UTC for each workspace.Bell notifications can include: 'N item(s) due today or tomorrow' with 'Publish now; nothing goes out automatically: <titles>', linking to the calendar; 'N item(s) waiting for you for over 2 days' with 'Approve or reject with a reason; Noor learns from your decision.', linking to the inbox; and 'N item(s) scored below 70' with the judge's note and 'Regenerate with a clearer note or edit before approving.', linking to the inbox. On an account with several brands the due and waiting titles end with the brand's name. Reem's overdue to-do notices come in the same run, and low-credit and trial-ending notices come the same way. The same follow-ups are also e-mailed, inside Noor's brief or on their own, depending on the e-mail preference.followUps(user, scope) reads the light task index and pushes notifications, deduplicated by fingerprint, unless follow-ups are switched off in the schedule. The daily route then sends the brief e-mail with the follow-ups attached (preference 'all', or 'important' when an item waits for a decision, a follow-up fired or a to-do is overdue or due today), or a follow-up-only e-mail titled 'Follow-up from your Thakeen team' when there is no brief (unless the preference is 'none').

Needs: Due, stale or weak items exist.

  • follow-ups off -> none
  • e-mail preference none -> bell only

Milestone celebration
any-signed-in
JourneyCard on /{lang}/app and /{lang}/app/chat
Reaches a launch-path milestone: approves an item, sets a publish date or publishes from the inbox, or finishes a milestone elsewhere (brand file, guard word, connection, AI link) and then sees the card.A confetti burst and a toast 'Milestone unlocked: {milestone}' for about 3.6 s. A level change shows 'Level up: Sapling', 'Level up: Palm' or 'Level up: Oasis' instead. On the first visit in a browser, only the brand milestone is celebrated. Under prefers-reduced-motion there is no confetti or animation.After a successful approve, date or publish, the inbox dispatches nashr:milestone so the card updates before the next server render. The card compares the done set and the level with localStorage nashr:journey:v1 and writes them back; if storage fails, a celebration may repeat. Points and levels are cosmetic and buy nothing.

The job is submitted once and never retried. fal answers inline: the result is copied to Blob when a store exists, the task is updated and track('image') runs. kie and Higgsfield answer 202 running. The result is also recorded in the activity log. A generator refusal (nsfw) is refunded with 422, a busy provider at submit is refunded with 429 busy, and any other failed submit is refunded with 502 media_failed. An opportunistic sweep runs after the response.

Wait while it renders
any-signed-in
/{lang}/app (MediaControls)
Stays on the page while a render runs. After a reload, the card picks up the running job again.The button stays disabled. Two minutes past the maximum age (10 min for images, 30 min for video) the card reads 'Not done yet. If it does not finish within minutes, the credits come back automatically and the reason shows here.' When done, the image or clip appears and the balance refreshes. When failed, it shows 'The render didn't work this time; all credits were returned.' or the specific reason. Later it shows 'The last render did not finish; N credits were returned.'The browser polls GET /api/media/{jobId}: every 3 s for images and 6 s for video, every 15 s after 2 minutes, and every 60 s once late. After an error or a 429 the gap doubles, up to 30 s. The route checks the session and reads the job only under sc(u) (404 otherwise). It allows 120 polls per minute (429 slow_down), then runs advance(), which makes at most one provider status call per 4 s under the job lock. When done, the result is copied to the Blob store, written to the task and the credits are kept. On failure or timeout, the provider job is cancelled where possible. The charge is then refunded exactly once, to the paying account (and its top-up pack) and the agent's counter, and mediaLast is recorded. Either end also writes a render_done or render_failed row to the activity log.

Needs: A job is running.

  • done -> media on card
  • failed/timeout -> refund + reason
  • late -> still-rendering note

Start OAuth connect
owner
/{lang}/app/integrations -> /api/connect/{provider}/start
Clicks Connect on an OAuth platform: X, Facebook or Instagram (Meta), Shopify, Salla, Zid, Meta Ads or Snapchat Ads. Shopify first asks for the store name in a dialog.The whole page goes to the platform's consent screen. A refusal before the redirect shows a raw JSON page: 401, 403 forbidden, 404 unknown_provider, 503 provider_not_configured, or 400 shop_required or bad_shop.GET /api/connect/{provider}/start?lang&shop checks the session, the 'connectors' role (admin and above) and that the OAuth provider is configured. A Shopify host is normalised to *.myshopify.com and validated. A random state is saved for 10 minutes with the scope, provider, lang, shop, the PKCE verifier (X only) and the acting login. The nashr_oauth cookie is set: httpOnly, SameSite lax, 10 min, path /api/connect. The route then answers with a 307 redirect to the authorize URL.

173
Return from platform
owner
Platform consent -> /api/connect/{provider}/callback -> /{lang}/app/integrations
Grants (or declines) access on the platform, which sends the browser back.The person lands on /{lang}/app/integrations?connected=<id>, which shows 'Connected: <id>' and the new card under Connected. A Meta card shows the chosen Page. When that Page has no Instagram professional account, it adds 'No Instagram professional account is linked to this Page, so posts go to Facebook only...'. On failure or a decline, the person lands on ?error=<code> with 'Verification failed. Check the details. (<code>)'. Codes include the platform's own error such as access_denied, invalid_state, session_mismatch, forbidden, bad_hmac, bad_shop, meta_no_page, no_ad_account, or a token error.GET /api/connect/{provider}/callback reads and clears the cookie and takes the state, which can be used once. It checks: the same provider; that the cookie equals the state (sameSecret); the same account; and the same acting login. It then re-checks the 'connectors' role, and for Shopify also the HMAC and the shop. It exchanges the code and reads the account name (X @user; Salla or Zid store). For Meta it gets a long-lived token, lists the Pages, and picks the first Page with Instagram, else the first Page. For ad providers it lists the ad accounts and picks the first. The connection is saved AES-256-GCM encrypted under the scope from the state, and track('connect') runs. The inbox then offers the matching Publish buttons. The way back to the integrations page is built on the configured app origin, never on the request's Host header.

Needs: The same browser and login that started the flow.

  • error=access_denied (declined)
  • error=session_mismatch / forbidden / invalid_state
  • error=meta_no_page

Publish to a platform
owner
/{lang}/app (Inbox card)
Clicks 'Publish to <platform>' on a card. The buttons show to admins and owners (a member is offered none) on any card whose type fits a connected target, including Rejected cards, which the server refuses. Articles go to WordPress or Shopify as drafts. X-channel social posts go to X. Social and cart-recovery posts on the Instagram or Facebook channel go to Facebook, and to Instagram only with a generated image. Social, gbp and whatsapp items can go to Telegram.The button reads 'Publishing…'. On success, the card shows 'Published <platform> ↗' linking to the live post, or 'Draft on <platform> ↗' for WordPress and Shopify. The status becomes Approved and the journey card ticks 'publish'. If the text has a slot like {{1}} or a direction like [VO], the buttons are disabled and the card reads 'Not published as written: it has slots such as {{1}} filled per recipient, or script directions such as [VO]. Copy it to where it is used.' An Instagram-channel post without an image shows 'Instagram takes no post without an image: generate one for this post and the Publish to Instagram button appears.'POST /api/publish/{id} {provider} checks, in order: the session; the 'publish' role (403); the task in scope (404); a provider (400); not rejected (409 rejected_item); the brand gate with publish:true (409 brand_violation); and that the provider is in targetsFor(connected) (400 wrong_target). publishOnce then takes the 70 s publock (409 publish_busy), re-reads and re-checks the item, and refuses a leftover attempt marker (publish_unknown) or a platform already published (already_published). It writes the marker and calls publishTask with a fresh token, refreshing it or throwing reconnect_required. WordPress and Shopify create drafts. X (first 280 characters), Telegram (first 4000), a Facebook feed or photo post, and Instagram (container, then publish) go live. It appends {provider,url,status,at} to task.published, sets status approved, and sets decidedAt when the item was pending. It then clears the marker and runs track('publish'). A 'publish' decision, with who approved, is recorded only when this approved a pending item, and every post that went out adds one 'published' row to the activity log (agent of the item's type, the acting person, via app, the platform). No credits and no model call, so it works while paused.

Needs: Owner or admin; platform connected; item not rejected; passes the publish-strength brand gate.

  • success -> post link on card
  • ask again -> see Confirm publish again
  • refused -> see Publish refused
