182
Confirm publish again
owner
/{lang}/app (Inbox card, yellow notice)
Reads the notice on the card and, where offered, chooses 'Publish again' or Cancel.The notice depends on what happened. Already published: 'This item was already published to <p>. Publish it again?', with a link to the earlier post. Unknown result (no answer, a 5xx other than 502, or a marker left from an earlier try): 'The publish to <p> ended without an answer, so the post may already be live. Check <p> first, then publish again if it is not there.' Only these two offer 'Publish again' and Cancel. Busy: 'A publish is already running. Wait a minute, then refresh.' Duplicate: '<p> refused this text because it was already posted there. Nothing went out and the connection is fine. Change the wording, then publish.' Earlier attempt live: '<p> says this text is already there, so the earlier attempt went live. It is now recorded on the item; nothing new was posted.' These three have no buttons.'Publish again' sends the POST again with again:true. That skips the once-per-platform and unknown-result checks, but keeps the lock and the role, rejection and brand gates. mayHavePosted treats timeouts, network errors, 5xx answers and unreadable answers as 'may have posted', and the marker is kept for 3 days. Only a clear refusal counts as 'not posted'. A duplicate answer after an unknown attempt is recorded as that earlier post (earlier_live): the item is marked approved, a pending item gets its decision record and every case gets the activity row the unknown attempt never wrote. Otherwise it is 409 duplicate_post.

Needs: Owner or admin.

  • Publish again -> repeat publish
  • Cancel -> notice closes

owner
/{lang}/app (Inbox card, browser alert)

This happens only when the brand file or the text changed after the page loaded, because otherwise the button is already disabled. 403 shows the alert 'Your role does not allow this.' Nothing is recorded on the item, and the attempt marker is cleared after a clear refusal.
  • 409 brand_violation -> held box
  • 403 -> role alert

11 Every day: morning jobs, brief, bell and emailopen diagram →

#StepThe person doesThey seeThe system doesCode
184
Morning job runs
system
/api/cron/daily (Vercel cron 0 5 * * *)
At 05:00 UTC (08:00 KSA), Vercel cron calls GET /api/cron/daily with the CRON_SECRET bearer.Nothing is visible yet. The person meets the results later: the brief card, bell items, emails, refreshed ad results, renewed credits, an expired top-up pack gone from storage and, when one was left unused, an added brand's setup charge back in the balance.sameSecret checks the bearer (401 otherwise). On the first hop only (no chain parameter) the job first sweeps Reem's due reminders, as the late-but-never-lost fallback for the QStash tick. dailyRoster then lists one item per account and active brand; every account has at least the default brand. It also lists idle scopes: archived brands, and every brand of a suspended account. runCronBatch runs 8 at a time. It keeps a per-day done marker and allows at most 2 attempts per workspace. It re-invokes itself when items were not started within its 240 s start budget or failed, up to 30 hops. For each workspace, dailyFor runs in this order. (1) renewIfDue: on a paid plan, the first cron run (or paid text batch, whichever comes first) of a new UTC month resets the month's plan credits to allowanceOf(u), with no rollover: Starter 300, Pro 700, Business 1,500, and Agency its deal's credits, or 1,500 without a deal at or above the floor. Top-up packs keep their own balance. A record still stored as Enterprise reads as Agency. Trial accounts are never renewed, and neither is a stored plan that is not recognised (it reads as trial). (2) On the default brand's entry only: saveSettledTopups writes the settled record when a top-up pack expired; the setup-charge sweep (sweepOnboardHolds); the 15% first-month offer on the trial's last day (grantEarlyOffer); and the Sunday-plan hold sweep (sweepWeeklyHolds). (3) secretaryDaily purges Reem's items deleted over 30 days ago, cleans her away list, warns about (a bell item opening My to-dos) and deletes closed copies of recurring series, and auto-closes items whose linked inbox item was approved. (4) Either the no-brand nudge, or the ad sync, lifecycle emails, brief, follow-ups and email. Idle scopes get only the purge. From Sunday to Tuesday (UTC), on the hop that does not chain further, the job also kicks /api/cron/weekly as a catch-up. The report counts briefs, emails, nudges and renewals, and says whether mail is configured. No model call is made anywhere in this job, so it runs unchanged while CLAUDE_PAUSED = true.

Needs: Every account in users:all, once per active brand

  • No brand file: signup-nudge path only
  • A top-up, setup-sweep, offer, plan-hold, to-do or ad-sync failure is recorded in the outcome and the rest of that workspace's day continues
  • Suspended account or archived brand: only the 30-day purge runs
  • Sunday to Tuesday UTC: weekly-plan catch-up kicked after the batch

Reem's reminders delivered
system
/api/cron/daily first hop (sweepDaily), bell and email
On the first hop of the daily job, before the workspaces run, Reem's reminders that are due are delivered.A bell item 'Reminder: «title»' ('You asked to be reminded of this. Due: …') opening My to-dos, and an email 'Reminder from Reem: …' unless the preference is 'None' or mail is not set up. A reminder normally arrives at its time through the QStash tick; this sweep is the late-but-never-lost fallback, so without the tick it arrives with the morning job.Only on the first hop (no chain parameter). sweepDaily calls sweepReminders with a 60 s budget (SWEEP_BUDGET_DAILY_MS) and repeats up to 4 more times while a pass read the full 200 scopes. Each pass reads up to 200 scopes with a due reminder from sec:duescopes and up to 50 ids per scope, grouped by account, 8 accounts at a time. A suspended account's scopes and a brand that is not active are held for 6 hours. For each id a 330 s lease is taken (SET NX), then fireReminder runs deliver under the item's lock: pushNotifs claims the fingerprint todo-remind:{scope}:{id}:{ms} (kept 7 days) and, only when that push was new, sends the email. The email needs RESEND_API_KEY, and the profile preference for the scope (else the account's) must not be 'none'. A failure leaves the id due for the next sweep; nothing new starts after the deadline. No model call, no credits. The sweep report is returned with the job's report.

Needs: First hop of the daily job; a reminder is due

  • Suspended account or archived brand: reminders held 6 hours

Unused setup refunded
system
/api/cron/daily (sweepOnboardHolds), on the account record user:{email}
On each account's default-brand entry, the daily job looks for added-brand setup charges (holds) that no setup run charged or reused for 24 hours.No email, bell item or message; only the balance changes. When the added brand was never built (its setup run was killed mid-run, or its own refund failed) and the owner did not retry, the 9 credits come back. The sidebar and billing show the new balance on the next page load, and that day's brief on the default brand counts it. When the brand was built, the charge stands and nothing changes. While paused (current), an added brand's setup answers 503 'paused' before any charge, so no new hold is made.Runs only when the entry is the default brand's and User.onboardPaid holds something. sweepOnboardHolds keeps each hold whose last charge or reuse (hold.at) is at least ONBOARD_HOLD_MS (24 h) old; a malformed time counts as old. It reads the brand file of each such scope side by side. resolveOnboardHolds then settles them all in one save under the user lock. A hold whose brand exists is dropped with no refund. Any other gives its credits back, lowers Noor's counter for that brand while still in the charge's month, and is dropped, and an onboard_refunded row is written to the brand's activity log for each refund (by the cron). The outcome records onboard_refunded:N and onboard_settled:N; a throw records onboard_sweep_failed and the rest of the day continues. Archived brands are covered, because the sweep runs per account; suspended accounts only get the idle purge, so they are not swept. No model call.

Needs: Default-brand entry of an account holding an added-brand setup charge that no run charged or reused for 24 hours

  • Suspended account: not swept

Sunday weekly plan
system
/api/cron/weekly (Vercel cron 0 3 * * 0), plus catch-ups kicked by the daily job Sunday to Tuesday
On Sunday at 03:00 UTC (06:00 KSA), the weekly cron asks Noor for the week's plan for every brand whose weekly plan is on.When the model is on: a bell item 'This week's plan is ready in your inbox' ('N item(s) from Noor. Approve what you like and set publish dates.', with the brand's name added on a multi-brand account), the plan items in the inbox, and 2 credits taken from the balance. An item is already approved when auto-approve covers weekly_plan and the brand gate passes. The first brand gets it by default; a brand added from the Brands page gets nothing on Sunday unless the owner switched the weekly plan on in Scheduled tasks. While paused (current state): nothing appears in any brand that week, no credit moves and nothing tells the person.Runs 4 brands at a time, with markers per Sunday period, and stops starting brands after 160 s. runWeekly checks, in order: no_brand; off (weekly plan switched off); already_this_week. getSchedule defaults weeklyPlan to on for the default brand and to off for an added brand (a scope containing '#'). On an added brand a stored weeklyPlan counts only with the weeklyPlanSetAt stamp that the owner's switch (PUT /api/schedule) writes. The model check comes next: while paused it returns 'retry:paused' before any charge. The attempt is not counted, so the week stays open for the catch-ups, which also answer paused. Then the weeklylock lock (retry:locked), trial_over, and the weekly cap: items the team made on its own in the last 7 days (isAutonomous: the Sunday plan, routines and Majed's auto-run) >= gov.weeklyCap (default 20) gives cap_reached. It then charges TASK_COST.weekly_plan, 2 credits, once per brand and week through the claim weekly:{scope}:{week}, held until the plan is saved, against Noor's monthly cap (agent_cap or no_credits, with the 80% warning). It generates weekly_plan with preferences, memory files, feedback and the reviewer, and refunds the 2 credits if that throws. Items are saved with initialStatus (auto-approve plus the brand gate), and judgeLater scores them after the response. lastWeekly is set when the batch is committed, and the notification uses fingerprint weekly:{date}. A charge whose run was killed is settled by the daily plan-hold sweep.

Needs: Brand exists; Weekly plan on (default on for the first brand, off for added brands); trial not over; at least 2 credits

  • Paused (current): retry:paused, no items, no charge, no notification
  • Added brand not switched on: 'off', nothing arrives and nothing is charged
  • Weekly plan off, trial over, cap reached, no credits or agent cap: skipped with no message to the person
  • Generation fails: the 2 credits are refunded

Routines run
system
/api/cron/routines (Vercel cron 0 6 * * *)
Every day at 06:00 UTC, the routines cron runs each routine the owner switched on that has not yet run this week or month.When the model is on: a bell item '<type> from <agent> is in your inbox' ('N item(s) from a routine you turned on. Review them and approve what fits.'), the items in the inbox, and the routine's cost taken from the balance (Dana's weekly report: 3 credits a run). While paused, idle or without data: nothing appears and nothing is charged.dueRoutines keeps routines that are on and have not run in their period: the UTC week from Sunday, or the UTC month. The same cron also runs Majed's auto-run for brands whose owner switched it on, first. Items run 4 at a time, start until 160 s, and the route re-invokes itself until everyone is served. runRoutine checks, in order: off; already_this_period; paused, which answers retry:paused and is asked again the next day; mock mode (no API key outside production: skip:mock); the lock (retry:locked); trial_over. Then a gate checked in code answers idle or no_data, at no charge, when its data is missing. Dana's weekly report needs owner-made items, decisions, or a store or ad sync in the last 7 days. Noor's cart recovery needs abandoned-cart data from a store sync newer than its last run. Razan's cash forecast needs store sales synced in the last 35 days. Majed's monthly review needs decisions on 3 or more items in the last 30 days. With less than 180 s of the invocation left it answers retry:late and charges nothing. The weekly cap is shared with the weekly plan and checked under a per-brand lock, counting this batch and other routines' held batches (cap_reached). At most 2 unsettled charges are allowed per period (attempts_exhausted). The run charges TASK_COST[type] against the agent's cap (agent_cap or no_credits): Dana's weekly report 3, Noor's cart recovery 3, Razan's cash forecast 4, Majed's monthly review 5, generates with the data blocks, and refunds if generation throws. Items are saved with source 'scheduled', judgeLater runs, the last run is set, and one notification goes out per routine and period.

Needs: Routine switched on for the brand (all are off by default)

  • Paused (current): retry:paused every day, nothing produced
  • Gate not met: idle or no_data, free
  • Cap reached, no credits or agent cap: skipped with no message
  • Under 180 s left: retry:late, handed to the next hop

The reminder arrives
system
Bell, email (no page action)
Nothing to do. The reminder time arrives.A bell notification 'Reminder: «title»' ('You asked to be reminded of this. Due: …') appears in the app at the set time, and an email 'Reminder from Reem: {title}' follows when the profile preference allows it. The row's reminder line goes and a 'reminded' note is added.A QStash tick calls /api/cron/secretary every 15 minutes, signed by QStash (Upstash-Signature) or with the CRON_SECRET bearer; only a QStash tick records sec:tick. sweepReminders reads the sec:duescopes and sec:due indexes up to the current time and takes a 330-second lease per due id before firing. Accounts that are suspended or archived are held for 6 hours. fireReminder delivers first: the bell (fingerprint todo-remind, so a retry never doubles it), then the email only when the bell was new, RESEND_API_KEY is set and the preference is not 'none'. Only then does it clear the reminder (remindAt null) and add a 'reminded' note, under the item's lock. If the ticker is down, the daily cron (05:00 UTC) sweeps the missed ones as a fallback, within a 60-second budget.

Needs: A reminder is set and its time has passed

  • Email off or no key: bell only
  • Ticker down: the daily sweep sends it late

Save governance
owner
/{lang}/app/governance
Changes auto-approve types, reviewer, cross-agent review, learning, em-dashes, the weekly cap, notify minutes, agent caps or Majed's auto-run switch (empty cap means no cap), then clicks 'Save'. Switching Majed's auto-run on first asks in a confirm dialog.A cap that is not a whole number shows 'A cap must be a whole number or empty. Fix the field marked in red, then save.' and nothing is sent. Cancelling the auto-run dialog leaves the switch off. Otherwise 'Saving…', then 'Saved', with the fields showing the stored, clamped values and a new row at the top of 'Change history' when something changed. Refusals: 'Majed's auto-run needs a monthly cap for at least one agent he hands work to...', the confirmation line, 'Another save is in progress. Try again in a moment.', and 'Could not save. Try again.'.PUT /api/governance: 401, then forbid('governance.write') (owner only, 403). saveGovAs takes a per-brand lock (busy 409 after about two seconds) and merges the body into the stored settings: auto-approve keys must be task types with boolean values; weeklyCap is clamped to 0-200, notifyMinutes to 0-1440, caps to 0-100000; null or empty removes a cap; only real booleans change the switches. Majed's auto-run can be on only while an agent he hands work to has a cap (400 auto_handoff_needs_cap), and switching it from off to on needs the literal confirmation the page sends after its dialog (400 auto_handoff_confirm). A save that changed something writes gov:{scope}, pushes one revision (who, what changed, before and after) onto govhist:{scope} and adds a 'Governance changed' row to the activity log; a save that changes nothing writes nothing. The later effects are these. (1) Auto-approve makes new items of those types start approved only when the brand gate passes (banned words, placeholders) and no cross-agent reviewer flagged them. The judge can send an auto-approved item it auto-fails back to the inbox, with a bell item. (2) The reviewer and em-dash settings apply on generation; cross-agent review adds Razan's or Majed's notes to money and strategy types. (3) Learning off stops writing edits and rejections to preferences, and stops feeding past decisions to the prompts. (4) The weekly cap limits the weekly plan, the routines and Majed's auto-run. (5) Agent caps are enforced in the ledger's charge, before the balance check. Noor's cap in an added brand also covers that brand's setup from its URL: POST /api/onboard reads gov.agentCaps.noor for the brand's scope, refuses early when the 9 credits would pass it, then charges the setup under it (402 agent_cap, which opens the cap modal and says nothing was used). A retry of a setup already paid for reuses that charge and is not checked again. The setup charge that crosses 80% of the cap sends the same cap warning as a batch. notifyMinutes is stored, but nothing outside this page reads it. While paused (current), settings save normally but have nothing to apply to: nothing is generated, and an added brand's setup is refused as paused before the cap check.

Needs: Account owner (governance.write)

  • Invalid cap: blocked in the browser
  • Not owner: 403
  • Auto-run on with no cap on an agent Majed hands work to: refused, nothing saved
  • Auto-run confirm cancelled: stays off
  • Another save in progress: 409 busy
  • Noor's cap in an added brand: can block that brand's setup (402 agent_cap)
  • Paused (current): saved, no generation to apply to

Nothing was charged.' with a 'View plans' link, and Go stays disabled; a 402 on Go opens the dialog too. In the Getting started thread a refused charge opens the dialog and Noor says 'Setup of this brand did not start, and no credits were used.' While the model is paused, chat answers 'The agents are paused for now, so I can't reply or create content yet. No credits were used.' and Ask Noor/reports show 'Content generation is paused for now. No credits were used.', with no dialog. So while paused the dialog comes only from the image and video buttons, an added brand's /start page, and a chat whose messages for the month are already used.The API answers 402 trial_over, 402 no_credits {need, have} or 402 agent_cap {agent, spent, cap}, and nothing is charged. limitFromError maps the error to a dialog kind, and chat returns a limit field. POST /api/chat checks in this order, all before the owner's message is saved or any model runs: trialOver (402 trial_over), the short-window ceiling (429 slow_down with a retry window, never touching the month's count), then reserveChatTurn, which answers 402 chat_allowance when the month's messages (the trial's 30 for the whole trial; Starter 250, Pro 650, Business 1,300 a month; a founder override takes precedence) are used and the owner has not switched on 'Continue on credits', and 402 chat_credits when the switch is on but the balance is under the 5-credit hold (chatOverflowHold). /api/onboard/confirm answers 402 no_credits or trial_over {need, credits} for an added brand the balance cannot cover, and /api/onboard answers 402 no_credits, trial_over or agent_cap before it charges; StartLaunch and ChatPanel map both with limitFromError. /api/generate returns 503 model_paused before the trial and credit checks, and /api/onboard returns 503 paused before its charge checks. Chat returns the paused reply before any tool runs. /api/onboard/confirm calls no model, so it still checks while paused. The image and video render routes check trialOver and still run while paused: only the model-written render prompt is replaced by a fixed one.

Continue chat on credits
owner
/{lang}/app/billing chat messages card
Flips 'Continue on credits when messages run out'.The switch saves and the card shows the new state. Off (the default): when the month's chat messages run out, chat pauses until the 1st of the month (UTC). On: chat continues on credits while the balance holds at least 5 credits, each message billed what it actually cost, at most 5. A refusal shows under the switch: 'Only the account owner can change this.', 'Not available on a trial.', 'Too many changes. Try again shortly.' or 'Could not save. Try again.'POST /api/billing/chat-overflow {on}. forbid billing (owner only), then a rate limit of 30 changes an hour per account (429 slow_down). Only a real boolean is accepted (400 bad_request), and a trial is refused (409 trial: a trial's 30 messages are for the whole trial). The flag chatOverflow is set or deleted under the user lock, so it cannot undo a balance change saved at the same time, and the route answers the new chat status. The gate itself is in reserveChatTurn and settleChatTurn, which hold chatOverflowHold credits (5) for a message past the allowance and settle it at its metered cost.

Needs: Owner on a paid plan

  • on -> chat continues on credits past the allowance
  • off -> chat paused until the 1st (UTC)

  • 429 slow_down -> too many changes
  • non-owner -> 403

Founder activates plan
founder
/{lang}/app/admin (AdminConsole, Users tab)
After payment, the founder does one of three things in the console: picks the account's plan (trial, starter, pro, business or agency), adds credits ('+ credits', negative to remove), or sets a seat override (0 clears it). Each action prompts for a reason ('+ credits' asks for the amount first). For Agency the terms are set next with 'Agency deal…'.The founder sees '{email} → {plan}', with a line about any team change, or '{email}: {error}' when refused; a '+ credits' amount beyond ±100,000 shows '{email}: bad_credits' instead of a success line, and an empty, zero or non-numeric amount sends nothing. The owner gets no notification or email. Before the account is read, addCredits must be a finite number within ±100,000 (400 bad_credits) and seats 1-500, null or 0 (400 bad_seats). Under the user lock a plan change sets the plan, the month's plan credits to allowanceOf(u) (trial 50, Starter 300, Pro 700, Business 1,500, Agency the deal's credits when it respects the floor, else 1,500) with setMonthly, which keeps any live top-up pack's credits, and renewedAt = the current month. Leaving Agency deletes the deal and, unless the request sets seats, the seat override, and clears the custom chat-message override unless the request sets chatAllowance. addCredits adds to the plan's credits with addMonthly and never touches a top-up pack, and never below 0. Any plan or seat change, or a dropped override, runs enforceSeats. An admin:log entry records the actor, the plan, credits, seats and deal before and after, the team change and the reason.

  • leave Agency -> deal, its seat override and the chat override cleared

  • bad seats -> 400 bad_seats
  • unknown email -> 404

Founder adds a top-up pack
founder
/{lang}/app/admin (AdminConsole, Users tab, Add top-up)
After a pack is paid, clicks 'Add top-up' in the account's detail panel (disabled on a trial) and enters the pack (100, 300 or 1,000 credits), the amount paid before VAT, the payment date and a reference. Void and refund are separate buttons on the pack.The founder sees the pack added for the account, or '{email}: {error}'. The owner gets no email; on the next load Subscription and Profile list the pack with its credits left and an expiry 90 days out, and the balance rises by the pack. A void removes the pack, its unspent credits, its payment and any commission; a refund removes the credits still left in a paid pack at once.POST /api/admin/topups {email, action: 'add', credits, paidSar, paidOn, ref, paymentId}, authenticated by adminActor. parseTopup checks the four fields before any read: 400 bad_pack, bad_paid, bad_date, bad_ref; the console's request id is checked too (400 bad_id), so a retry adds one pack and records one payment. A trial is refused (409 topup_needs_paid_plan), so is a 51st live pack (409 too_many_topups) and an id whose payment was voided (409 payment_voided). addPaidTopup puts the pack on the account (valid 90 days from now) and records the payment with the partner's commission when one is due. getUser settles expired packs on every read, so a pack leaves the balance the moment it expires. spendCredits uses the plan credits first, then the packs that expire soonest.

Needs: Founder console session or console key; the account is on a paid plan

  • add -> pack on the account + payment recorded
  • trial -> 409 topup_needs_paid_plan
  • bad pack, amount, date or ref -> 400
  • void -> pack and payment removed
  • refund -> unused credits removed

264
Monthly credit reset
system
Cron (no page); visible in sidebar, Subscription and Profile
On its first run of a new UTC month, the daily cron resets paid plan credits; the first paid text batch of the month does the same for that account.A paid account's plan credits are back at the allowance: Starter 300, Pro 700, Business 1,500, and Agency the deal's credits (1,500 without a valid deal). Unused plan credits do not roll over; top-up packs keep what is left in them until they expire. Profile reads 'Your plan includes {allowance} credits per month. The plan's credits reset on the 1st of each month (UTC); top-up packs keep what is left in them until they expire. Next reset: {date}.' The chat message count restarts with the month. No email or notification is sent.The daily cron (bearer CRON_SECRET, 05:00 UTC) calls renewIfDue under the user lock; renewalDue also runs before the charge of every paid text batch, so whichever comes first renews. Trial accounts are never renewed, nor a stored plan the app cannot recognise (it reads as trial). When renewedAt (or the signup month) is not the current month, setMonthly sets the plan credits to allowanceOf(u), leaving the live top-up packs untouched, and renewedAt = that month. allowanceOf uses an Agency deal only while it respects the floor (at least 999 SAR and 1,500 credits, and not cheaper per credit than Business); otherwise Agency gets Business's 1,500. Expired top-up packs are settled whenever getUser reads the account (settleTopups), so a pack past its 90 days leaves the balance at once; the same cron run saves that settled record (saveSettledTopups).

Needs: plan is not trial

  • trial -> no renewal
  • already renewed this month -> no-op
  • Agency without a valid deal -> 1,500
  • top-up packs -> keep their credits until they expire

Members over the limit are paused only at the next refit: POST /api/admin/plan runs enforceSeats when the founder changes the plan or the seats, or when leaving Agency or clearing the deal drops the seat override (unless the same request sets seats). enforceSeats pauses the newest enabled members over the limit, cancels the newest pending invites that no longer fit, and when the limit grows brings paused members back oldest-first into seats no invite holds. Send invite
owner
/{lang}/app/team invite form
Types an email, picks Member or Admin, and clicks 'Send invite' ('Create invite link' when email is not set up).'Sending…', then 'Invite sent to {email}.' and the pending list reloads. With email off, a box shows the one-time link with a Copy button. Errors: 'That email is not valid.', 'This email cannot be invited. It may already be registered or have a pending invite.', 'No free seat (X of Y).', 'Too many attempts in a short time. Try again shortly.', 'The invitation email could not be sent, so no invitation was created. Try again shortly.', 'Your role does not allow this.'POST /api/team/invite. forbid team.invite.member. Rate limits: 20 a day per account (5 on trial), 30 an hour per IP, and 5 a day per invited address (hashed); these are checked before the address is validated. In createInvite, an admin invite needs the owner, the actor must still hold their role, and the owner's or the inviter's own address is refused (unavailable). Under lock:team, an existing login anywhere or a pending invite in this team gives 409 unavailable, and a full team gives 409 seats. The token is 32 random bytes; only its SHA-256 is stored (invite:{hash}, 7 days). The link is {origin}/{lang}/app/join#t=token, where the origin is the canonical app origin (APP_ORIGIN, else the production host), not the request's Host header. With mail on (RESEND_API_KEY and MAIL_FROM), a fixed-template email ('You are invited to join a team on Thakeen', or the Arabic equivalent) naming only the inviter's address goes to the invitee. If sending fails, or Resend gives no answer within 10 seconds (EMAIL_TIMEOUT_MS), the invite is withdrawn (502 send_failed). The account's bell, seen by every login, gets 'A new invitation was sent' (or 'A new invitation link was created'); it names the inviter but not the invited address or the role.

Needs: Owner (either role) or admin (members only); not while impersonating

  • mail on -> emailed
  • mail off -> link shown once
  • send failed or no answer in 10 s -> 502, no invite
  • 409 unavailable / seats
  • 429 rate limited
  • admin inviting admin -> forbidden

The app then calls /api/oauth/token: the code is redeemed once, PKCE is verified, and the app gets a 30-day access token, a 90-day refresh token and a grant record (mcpgrant). Refreshing rotates both tokens.

Use Noor from Claude
owner
Claude / ChatGPT (MCP client) calling /api/mcp
Asks their own assistant to use the Thakeen tools: get_brand_rules, check_brand, save_draft, list_inbox, read_file, list_files, the to-do tools (agenda_get, agenda_next, todo_create, todo_update, todo_propose_done), ask_noor and generate_content.While paused, ask_noor answers 'The agents are paused for now, so I can't reply or create content yet. No credits were used.' (the message count taken for it is given back) and generate_content returns the tool error 'Content generation is paused for now. No credits were used.' The free tools still work: brand rules, brand checks, drafts saved to the approvals inbox as pending, inbox and file reads, and the to-do tools. Normally ask_noor answers like the web chat: the message is trimmed and capped at 4,000 characters (empty: 'Empty message. Nothing was sent.'), it counts toward the account's monthly chat messages, and a low balance of messages is appended ('[chat: N of M messages left this month]'). When the messages are used up the connected app is refused with 'Chat paused: this account used its N chat messages this month (it renews on the 1st, UTC). Nothing was sent.' ('for the trial' on a trial) plus what the owner can do (upgrade, or turn on "continue on credits" on the Subscription page; on a trial, subscribe). With continue on credits on but fewer credits than the most one message can cost, the refusal says continuing on credits needs at least that many credits and the owner can top up or upgrade; otherwise, the message goes ahead and the reply says it was billed N credits. An ended trial or the hourly/daily chat limit gives the same refusal text as the web chat. generate_content charges the task's credits and adds items to the inbox; weekly_plan takes 2 credits, weekly_report 3 and followup_seq 4.POST /api/mcp (JSON-RPC, server name 'thakeen'). initialize and tools/list need no auth. A 401 carries WWW-Authenticate with the canonical origin (appOrigin()). Every tools/call resolves the bearer with userFromBearer (owner accounts only, scoped to the owner's active brand). Each tool is mapped to a role action in MCP_TOOL_ACTION (agenda_next included), and unknown tools are refused. ask_noor applies the web chat's gates in order: trialOver, chatRefusal (hourly and daily limits), then reserveChatTurn (the shared monthly allowance, or credits when continue on credits is on). It then runs chat inside runChatTurn with via 'mcp', so it never publishes or rewrites core memory, and the turn is settled after the reply. generate_content checks the pause before brand, trial and credits; when the model is on it charges TASK_COST[type] under the agent's monthly cap.

Needs: Valid personal token or OAuth grant

  • no or revoked token -> Unauthorized
  • empty message -> tool error
  • trial over, hourly or daily limit -> chat refusal text
  • monthly chat messages used up -> 'Chat paused' (or billed on credits when continue on credits is on)
  • continue on credits on but too few credits -> 'Chat paused' with the credits needed
  • paused -> paused text, no charge
  • trial over (when not paused) -> tool error
  • agent cap or too few credits (when not paused) -> tool error
  • too many drafts an hour -> save_draft refused

Setup nudge email
system
Email (daily cron /api/cron/daily)
Runs automatically at 05:00 UTC every day for an account that signed up but has no brand yet.The person receives one email, "Noor is ready — give her your website" (in Arabic for Arabic accounts), with a link to /{lang}/app/start. Nothing appears in the app.The daily cron checks the CRON_SECRET bearer with sameSecret and runs dailyFor for every active brand scope of every non-suspended account. With no brand, an account older than 10 minutes and mailReady() (RESEND_API_KEY and MAIL_FROM both set), it calls sendOnce(nudge1): a 120-second claim so two cron runs cannot both send, then sendEmail (10 s timeout), and the long once-marker (a dated record, 400 days) is written only after the provider accepted the message. A refused or timed-out send releases the claim and is tried again on the next run while the account still has no brand; the cron reports it as mail_refused:nudge and admin:log gets mail:refused (the recipient is not recorded). A marker burned by the old claim-before-send guard before 2026-10-01 17:00 UTC is cleared once and the nudge goes out again. The text says Thakeen and the link uses the app origin. While the model is paused, /api/onboard answers "paused", so an owner who tries to build the brand through onboarding stays without a brand and on this branch.

Needs: No brand; account older than 10 minutes; mailReady() (RESEND_API_KEY and MAIL_FROM); not suspended

  • RESEND_API_KEY or MAIL_FROM missing -> no email attempted
  • already sent (confirmed marker) -> no email
  • provider refuses or times out -> logged as mail:refused, retried on the next run
  • another run holds the claim -> skipped this run
  • suspended account -> skipped

314
Founder key sign-in
founder
/{lang}/app/admin?login=1 (AdminLogin)
Opens /{lang}/app/admin?login=1 without a founder session, types the admin key in 'Admin key' and clicks 'Enter'.With the right key the page refreshes into the founder console. With a wrong key, 'Wrong key.' appears under the field. After too many tries it shows 'Too many attempts. Try again in 15 minutes.', and 'Could not sign in right now. Try again.' for any other failure. Without ?login=1 the page is a plain 404, so the console stays hidden.POST /api/admin/login. It allows 5 attempts per 15 minutes per IP and answers 429 slow_down after that. The key is compared in constant time with WAITLIST_ADMIN_KEY (401 otherwise). On success createAdminSession sets a random session cookie and stores a server record adminsession:{sha256 of the id} bound to the key's fingerprint. The session ends after 15 minutes without a console request (ADMIN_SESSION_IDLE) or 12 hours at most (ADMIN_SESSION_MAX), on "End key session" (DELETE /api/admin/login), or when the key is rotated; each console request renews it at most once a minute. isAdminCookie() then opens the page, and an expired session makes the console's calls answer 401 (adminFetch announces it and the console shows that the session ended).

Needs: Knows the admin key. Not needed for a founder signed in as themselves.

  • wrong key
  • 6th try in 15 min: 429
  • no ?login=1: 404
  • key not configured: always refused
  • 15 min idle or 12 h: session ends, console calls 401

Clicks 'Founder' in the sidebar or the account menu, or opens /{lang}/app/admin. Then switches between the Overview, Users, Leads, Usage, Billing, Partners, Quality, Wishlist and Audit log tabs. On a key session, 'End key session' ends it.Top tiles: Users, Paying, signed up today, Trial, model cost this month, tokens, model calls and Suspended. Paying and Trial count unsuspended accounts only, so a suspended account still counts in Users (and signed up today) and in Suspended, but not in Paying or Trial. The Leads tab shows a count of unread sales leads. Overview tiles: Users, New (7d), Activated (brand file) with its rate, Active today, Active this week, Active this month, Stickiness DAU/MAU, 7-day retention, MRR (SAR, est.), 'Top-ups this month (SAR, excl. VAT, not in MRR)', Credits used (month), Model cost (USD, month) and Errors (7d); below them the events per day for the last 14 days, the 30-day funnel (signup, activated, first batch, first approval, paid) and the recent users. Billing shows MRR, Top-ups this month, ARPU, model and render cost and gross margin, render spend by model and provider, and the plan mix. MRR sums the monthly price of every unsuspended account: 0 for a trial, 299, 499 or 999 SAR by plan, or an Agency account's deal price (999, as Business, without a deal); top-ups are not in MRR; ARPU is MRR over the paying count. Quality shows agent quality from the last 30 days and the golden set. Audit log shows the last 50 founder actions with before → after (plan, credits, seats, deal, chat allowance, top-ups and payments) and the reason. Everyone else gets a plain 404, including customers, members and a founder who is impersonating. When a console call answers 401 the console is replaced by 'The key session has ended: 15 minutes passed without activity, or it reached 12 hours. Enter the key again to continue.' with 'Enter the key' (to ?login=1), or for a founder login 'Your founder console session has ended. Sign in again to continue.' with 'Sign in'.The page checks isAdminCookie(): either a live key session (random cookie, 15 minutes idle, 12 hours at most), or isFounderActor (the owner login itself, its email in ADMIN_EMAILS, not impersonating). On load the console calls GET /api/admin/plan, /api/admin/evals, /api/admin/overview, /api/admin/log and /api/admin/leads?limit=0 (the unread count). Each route answers 401 without isAdmin, and a 401 makes the console show that the session ended. /api/admin/plan returns each account's deal, allowance (allowanceOf), priceSar (priceOf), chat allowance and top-up credits left (topupCredits; the packs themselves come with the user pane), and the margin column uses SAR_PER_USD from src/lib/pricing.ts. overview() drops suspended accounts before counting paying and MRR (MRR = sum of priceOf). It only reads: no credits and no model calls.

Needs: Founder account signed in as itself, or a live admin key session.

  • not a founder: 404
  • founder impersonating: 404
  • key session idle 15 min or past 12 h: calls answer 401, console shows the session ended
  • tab still loading: 'Loading…'

317
Change plan, seats or credits
founder
/{lang}/app/admin, Users tab (plan dropdown, seats and credits controls) -> POST /api/admin/plan
In the Users table picks a plan in the plan dropdown (trial, starter, pro, business or agency), or sets the seat count, or clicks '+ credits'. Each then asks 'Reason (logged)'. The seats prompt comes first and takes 0 to clear the override; 'Credits to add (negative to remove):' comes first for credits and is filled with 100.A line '{email} → {plan}' appears, followed by any team change ('paused: …', 'back: …', 'invites cancelled: n'), or '{email}: seats N', or '{email} +N credits'. The table reloads with the new plan, balance and seat limit. A bad seat count shows '{email}: bad_seats' and nothing is sent; a server refusal shows its code, e.g. '{email}: bad_credits'. Cancelling the seats or credits prompt does nothing. Cancelling the reason prompt does not stop a change: it is sent with an empty reason, and a plan picked in the dropdown is always sent.POST /api/admin/plan authenticates with adminActor (401). A plan in the body sets the plan under the user lock, resets the month's plan credits to allowanceOf (the plan's credits, or the Agency deal's) with setMonthly, keeps live top-up packs, and stamps renewedAt with this month. Leaving Agency clears the deal and, unless the same request sets them, the seat override and the chat override. seats sets or clears the founder seat override (a whole number 1 to MAX_SEAT_OVERRIDE, 500; 0 or null clears it). addCredits takes a finite number of at most MAX_ADD_CREDITS (100,000) either way, truncated to a whole number, and adds it to or takes it from the month's plan credits only, never from top-up packs (400 bad_credits otherwise). A seat change, a plan change or a seat reset runs enforceSeats, which pauses the newest members over the limit, cancels invites that no longer fit, or brings paused members back. Every change is written to admin:log with plan, credits, seats, chat allowance and deal before and after, the team change and the reason. It records no customer payment: that is the Payments block, which also drives partner commission.

Needs: Founder console access.

  • bad_seats / bad_credits -> caught or refused, nothing written
  • fewer seats than members -> newest members paused, invites that no longer fit cancelled
  • plan changed -> the month's plan credits reset to the new allowance, top-up packs kept
  • plan changed away from agency -> deal, seat override and chat override cleared unless set in the same request

318
Set an Agency deal
founder
/{lang}/app/admin, Users tab ('Agency deal…' beside an Agency row's plan) -> POST /api/admin/plan
On a row whose plan is agency, clicks 'Agency deal…'. Types the monthly credits (1,500 to 100,000; filled with the current deal's or 1,500), then the monthly price in SAR excluding VAT (at least 999 and never cheaper per credit than Business; filled with the current deal's or 999), then the seats, owner included (1-500; filled with the current seat override, if any; empty keeps the current number), then 'Reason (logged)'. On an account that already has a deal, a confirm 'Apply the new deal now?' comes between the price and the seats. Leaving the credits empty clears the deal and goes straight to the reason.A line '{email}: Deal 3,000 @ 2,000 SAR' appears ('Deal no deal: Business' after a clear), followed by any team change ('paused: …', 'back: …', 'invites cancelled: n'), and the table reloads with the new deal, seat limit and margin. A typo shows '{email}: bad_deal', '{email}: below_floor' or '{email}: bad_seats' and nothing is sent; a server refusal shows its code, e.g. '{email}: deal_needs_agency'. Cancelling the credits, price or seats prompt does nothing. The owner gets no email or bell item. The month's plan credits become the deal's credits at once (live top-up packs are kept) on a first deal or when the founder chose OK, otherwise at the next monthly renewal, and Profile shows the deal's credits as 'Your plan includes N credits per month'. Profile never shows who set the deal or when.The browser checks the numbers with parseDeal (whole numbers within the bounds, credits at least 1,500, price at least 999 and price × 1,500 ≥ credits × 999) and sends {email, deal, plan:'agency' on a first deal or on OK, seats when typed, reason}, or {email, deal:null, reason} to clear. POST /api/admin/plan authenticates with adminActor (401), checks seats (400 bad_seats), parses the deal again (400 bad_deal or below_floor) and reads the account (404 not_found). Under the user lock it refuses a deal when the resulting plan (the plan in the body, else the stored one) is not agency (400 deal_needs_agency, nothing written), so a request carrying plan:'agency' is never refused and puts the account on Agency. Otherwise it stores u.deal {credits, priceSar, setAt, setBy}. With plan it also resets the month's plan credits to allowanceOf (the deal's credits) with setMonthly, keeping live top-up packs, and stamps renewedAt with this month. deal:null deletes the deal and, with it, a chat allowance override (chat goes back to the plan's messages), and, when there was one and the request does not set seats, the seat override with it, so seats go back to Business's 7; the balance is kept until the next renewal, which grants Business's 1,500. A seat change, a plan in the body or that seat reset runs enforceSeats, which pauses the newest members over the limit, cancels pending invites that no longer fit, or brings paused members back into free seats. admin:log records what changed (e.g. 'plan:agency deal:3000@2000 seats:20') with plan, credits, seats, chat allowance and deal before and after, the team change and the reason. From then on allowanceOf and priceOf use the deal for renewal, Profile, MRR, the margin column and the CSV. A stored deal below the Business rate is ignored, and the account then counts as Business (1,500 credits, 999 SAR). GET /api/profile returns only the deal's credits and price, never setAt or setBy.

Needs: Founder console access. The row's plan is agency: the link shows only there.

  • first deal -> sent with plan agency: balance = deal credits now, renewal cycle restarts
  • changed deal, OK -> applied now
  • changed deal, Cancel -> balance kept, new allowance at the next renewal
  • credits left empty -> deal and its seat override cleared, balance kept, account counts as Business
  • bad_deal / below_floor / bad_seats -> caught in the browser, nothing sent
  • deal sent without plan (changed deal, Cancel) while the account is no longer on agency -> 400 deal_needs_agency, nothing written
  • fewer seats than members -> newest members paused, invites that no longer fit cancelled

320
Add, void or refund a top-up
founder
/{lang}/app/admin, user pane (Top-ups block) -> POST /api/admin/topups
In a paid account's detail pane, in the Top-ups block, clicks 'Add top-up' and answers the prompts: 'Pack, in credits (100 / 300 / 1,000):' (filled with 100), 'Amount paid in SAR, excluding VAT:' (filled with the pack's list price, 1 SAR per credit), 'Payment date (YYYY-MM-DD, the day in Riyadh, not after today):' (filled with today in Riyadh), 'Payment reference (bank transfer or receipt number; optional):' and 'Reason (logged)'. On a listed pack clicks 'Void' (after a confirm), or 'Refund unused' on a paid pack with credits left: a confirm showing the amount to transfer (credits left × the price per credit paid, excluding VAT), 'Transfer date (YYYY-MM-DD, the day in Riyadh, not after today):', 'Transfer reference (bank transfer number; required):' and the reason. 'Finish refund' appears on a pack whose refund stopped before its payment was marked.A line '{email}: Top-ups +N → <balance>' appears, with ' · commission SAR x' when a partner earns one on it; the block lists each pack with credits left, expiry (90 days), amount paid, date, reference and who added it, or 'No live top-up pack.'. On a trial the 'Add top-up' button is disabled with 'Top-ups are sold to paid plans only: change the plan first.'. Void shows '{email}: Top-ups Void −N → <balance>'; a refund shows '{email}: Refund unused −N · x SAR → <balance>' and the pack then reads 'refunded N credits · x SAR on <date>'. A bad pack or amount shows '{email}: bad_pack' or '{email}: bad_paid' and nothing is sent; an empty transfer reference shows '{email}: bad_ref'; a pack whose credits left changed shows 'left_changed: the pack now has N credits left (x SAR); nothing was refunded. Check the amount and try again.'. Cancelling any prompt but the reason does nothing. The pane and the Payments block reload.POST /api/admin/topups {email, action: add | void | refund} authenticates with adminActor (401) and parses the pack again (parseTopup: a listed pack, amount, date, reference; paymentId pay_<hex>). addPaidTopup under the account lock refuses a trial (409 topup_needs_paid_plan) and a 51st live pack (409 too_many_topups, at most 50), adds a credit lot valid for 90 days and records the pack's payment and, when one is due, the partner's commission (15% within the partner's window) with it; a repeated click of the same pack reuses one paymentId so a retry cannot add it twice. voidPaidTopup voids the payment and the commission first, then removes the lot's unspent credits. refundPaidTopup takes the credits the console showed (409 left_changed when they changed), a transfer date and a required reference, records the refund (refundId rf_…) on the pack's payment and lowers the commission in proportion; finish:true completes an interrupted refund from the pack's own refund record. A top-up never uses the 15% first-month offer. Each action is logged to admin:log.

Needs: Founder console access. A paid account.

  • trial account -> button disabled; server 409 topup_needs_paid_plan
  • 51st live pack -> 409 too_many_topups
  • bad_pack / bad_paid / bad date -> nothing written
  • void -> payment and commission voided, unspent credits removed
  • refund unused -> credits left removed, refund on the payment, commission lowered in proportion
  • credits left changed since the confirm -> 409 left_changed, nothing refunded
  • empty transfer reference -> bad_ref, nothing sent
  • interrupted refund -> Finish refund completes it
  • retry of the same add -> one pack only

Record or void a payment
founder
/{lang}/app/admin, user pane (Payments block) -> POST /api/admin/payments/{email}
In a user's detail pane, in the Payments block, picks 'Plan' (starter, pro, business or agency), fills 'Amount SAR excl. VAT' (the expected price is the placeholder), 'Date' (today in Riyadh) and 'Reference', and clicks 'Record payment'. On a listed payment clicks 'Void' and answers 'Why is this payment voided (refund, mistake)?'.The block lists each payment with its date, plan (or 'top-up N'), amount with any refund, the first-month discount used, the commission ('commission SAR x') or why not ('not counted': no partner, self-referral, same team, paid before the referral, first payment after 30 days, after the first 4 months, refunded in full), and the reference. A 'Partner' line names the code, partner, 'by link' or 'by code', the date and the status. Before the first payment it says the 15% first-month offer is unused (with the expected first month per plan and 'It can be used until <day>'), used, or ended on its last day ('no longer shown to the customer'). A voided payment shows 'voided'. A top-up whose pack is live shows 'void it with its pack (Top-ups)'. After a click the line reads 'record ✓' or 'void ✓', or 'Failed: <code>'.GET /api/admin/payments/{email} (isAdmin) returns paymentsView; POST uses adminActor and calls recordPayment or voidPayment. recordPayment stores the plan payment (SAR excluding VAT) as the source of truth for partner commission: 15% of each payment for the first 4 months within 30 days of the referral, for a partner that is not the account itself or on its team; the plan's first payment also uses the account's unused first-month offer (the early-partner or partner-code 15%, until its last day). voidPayment keeps the record, marks it voided and takes its commission back; voiding the first plan payment gives the first-month offer back to the next one. A top-up payment whose pack is live is refused (409 topup_live). Either action re-takes the account's commission decisions and logs the ones that changed as reclassified. Neither changes the account's plan or credits: that is done in the Users table. Logged to admin:log.

Needs: Founder console access.

  • no partner / self / same team / before attribution / lapsed / after 4 months -> payment recorded, no commission
  • void -> payment and commission voided
  • first payment -> uses the first-month offer
  • void of the first payment -> the offer goes back to the next one
  • top-up payment with a live pack -> 409 topup_live, void it with its pack instead

/{lang}/app/admin, Partners tab (PartnersPanel) -> /api/admin/partners

Commissions come from the recorded payments (15% for the first 4 months within 30 days of the referral). Bank-detail changes by an impersonating founder are refused in code. Each action is logged to admin:log.

  • balance below SAR 200 or over the payable balance: refused
  • bank details unconfirmed or changed since loaded: refused
  • void payout: amount back in the balance

/{lang}/app/admin, Billing tab
Clicks 'Export users CSV'.A new tab downloads nashr-users-YYYY-MM-DD.csv. It is UTF-8 with a BOM so Excel shows Arabic correctly. Columns: email, name, phone, plan, credits, trial_ends, created_at, renewed_at, monthly_price_sar (299, 499 or 999 by plan, 0 for a trial, or the Agency deal's price; 999 for Agency without a deal), monthly_credits (the plan's allowance or the deal's credits), chat_allowance (the founder override, else the plan's messages), seats (the seat limit, owner included) and deal ('credits@price', empty without one).GET /api/admin/export (401 without isAdmin) reads every account in users:all in parallel with pmap and getUser (which maps a stored 'enterprise' plan to agency), then builds the CSV with usersCsv: priceOf, allowanceOf, chatAllowanceOf, seatLimit and the deal, every cell through csvSafe.

Needs: Founder console access.

  • not admin: 401 JSON

Run golden set
founder
/{lang}/app/admin, Quality tab (GoldenPanel)
Clicks 'Run now' on 'Golden set · weekly', or 'Finish missing cases' when the latest run is partial. Clicks Show or Hide on a case to read its items.In a normal run the button reads 'Running… up to 5 minutes, keep this tab open', then the table refreshes with scores per case and any regressions. The panel shows 'Another run is in progress.' when locked and 'Run failed: …' on other errors. While paused (today), 'Run now' is disabled and the panel says 'Claude is paused in code (src/lib/model.ts), so the set cannot run.' Without a key it says 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is not set here, so the set cannot run.'POST /api/admin/golden {resume} (needs isAdmin, 300 s). runGolden('manual') returns nothing unless modelState() is on; it takes the golden lock (300 s), runs fixed cases on fictional brands through agent → reviewer → judge and meters the cost under the golden account. It stores golden:run:{id}, compares it with the previous run of the same version, and logs to admin:log. It answers 409 already_running, model_paused or no_model_key.

Needs: Founder console access. Model on (CLAUDE_PAUSED false).

  • paused: disabled
  • no key
  • already running
  • partial run: resume

Weekly golden check
system
/api/cron/golden, then the founder's bell
No action. On Saturday at 01:00 UTC (04:00 Riyadh), Vercel cron calls GET /api/cron/golden with the CRON_SECRET bearer.When a run finds regressions, each founder account gets a quality bell item in Arabic, 'المجموعة الذهبية: N تنبيه، المتوسط X', linking to /ar/app/admin. While paused (today) nothing runs and no bell item is sent.The bearer is checked with sameSecret. runGolden('cron') returns nothing when the model is not on or the golden lock is held, and the cron answers 409 locked_or_no_model_key. Otherwise it runs and stores the run. On regressions, notifyItem goes to every ADMIN_EMAILS account with fingerprint golden:{id}:{n}, kept for 7 days.

Needs: CRON_SECRET set. Model on.

  • paused or locked: 409, nothing sent
  • no regressions: no bell

End the key session
founder
/{lang}/app/admin, top of the console (End key session) -> DELETE /api/admin/login
On a console opened with the admin key, clicks 'End key session'.The browser goes to the marketing home /{lang}. Reopening /{lang}/app/admin without ?login=1 is a 404; the key form is again at ?login=1.DELETE /api/admin/login calls destroyAdminSession: the server record is removed, so the same cookie never opens the console again, and any impersonation session opened from it ends with it. The button is shown only for a key session, not for a founder signed in as themselves. A key session also ends by itself after 15 minutes without a console request or 12 hours, or when the key is rotated.

Needs: A live admin key session.

  • founder signed in as themselves: no button
  • idle 15 min / 12 h: the same end, without a click
